# Image

This guide describes the public Go APIs and their contracts against the pinned Gophercloud and Python openstacksdk sources. Support decisions apply to each operation below.

See the [support ledger](../docs/sdk-support-ledger.md) for status definitions and shared limitations.

## Operation contracts

### `python:image/v2/find_image`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image/v2/images.API.FindIdentity`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/resource.WithIdentityFindOptions`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/resource.WithIdentityFindIgnoreMissing`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/resource.WithIdentityFindFallback`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/resource.WithIdentityFindQuery`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.Service.FindImageRecord`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithFindImageRecordOpts`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithFindImageRecordHeader`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithFindImageRecordHeaders`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithFindImageRecordIgnoreMissing`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithFindImageRecordListOptions`

- The pinned direct proxy accepts a string name_or_id and ignore_missing=True. Safe strings use native GET first; only compatible GET400/403/404 fall back to the normal list, with explicit strict, 404-only and GET-only Go options. Root-object GET200 extraction preserves the native Image model, additional Properties and import/store response headers.
  Tests: `api/find_identity_images_test.go:TestNativeFindIdentityImagesGETContractsAndNativeModel`, `api/find_identity_images_test.go:TestNativeFindIdentityImagesMissingDefaultsAndFallbackBoundaries`.
- Only a complete successful normal-list absence triggers a second native LIST. It starts from the frozen caller query, overlays mandatory os_hidden=true, omits the automatic name hint and never repeats GET. Explicit name filters and repeated/nil/empty query-key precedence survive; caller hidden=false is overridden only for that second search.
  Tests: `resource/find_identity_missing_list_test.go:TestCollectionFindIdentityMissingListWaitsForCompleteSuccessfulAbsence`, `resource/find_identity_missing_list_test.go:TestCollectionFindIdentityMissingListPreservesCallerQueryAndMandatoryOverlay`, `api/find_identity_images_test.go:TestNativeFindIdentityImagesHiddenPassUsesOriginalCallerQuery`.
- Both searches consume all native ImagePage continuations and compare exact ID OR name. Repeated-ID duplicates, malformed whole-page native extraction, invalid result IDs, cycle, late HTTP/decode errors and cancellation remain terminal. Normal errors never start the hidden pass; list403/404 are never ignored.
  Tests: `api/find_identity_images_test.go:TestNativeFindIdentityImagesNormalSearchTerminalNeverRetriesHidden`, `api/find_identity_images_test.go:TestNativeFindIdentityImagesHiddenPagesAndTerminalFailures`, `resource/find_identity_missing_list_test.go:TestCollectionFindIdentityMissingListErrorsAndCancellationAreTerminal`.
- IgnoreMissing is applied only after both lists complete successfully without a match; strict absence has no suppressed GET400/403 cause. GET-only never performs either list. Unsafe names retain their literal list-only policy; SDK control keys, unsupported details/all_projects and canceled/nil context fail before HTTP.
  Tests: `api/find_identity_images_test.go:TestNativeFindIdentityImagesMissingDefaultsAndFallbackBoundaries`, `api/find_identity_images_test.go:TestNativeFindIdentityImagesPreflightUnsafeNamesAndCancellation`, `resource/find_identity_missing_list_test.go:TestCollectionFindIdentityMissingListStrictAndFallbackPolicies`, `resource/find_identity_missing_list_test.go:TestCollectionFindIdentityMissingListPolicyPreflightAndOtherOperationsStayUnchanged`.
- SDK policy metadata and caller options are deep snapshots, including callback mutation boundaries and concurrent reuse. Generated leaf and handwritten Image collections preserve reverse ResourceBase, original live provider token, headers, HTTP middleware and caller context across GET and both searches. Ordinary Get/List/explicit Ref lookup are unchanged.
  Tests: `resource/find_identity_missing_list_test.go:TestCollectionFindIdentityMissingListSnapshotsAndConcurrentReuse`, `resource/find_identity_missing_list_test.go:TestCollectionFindIdentityMissingListFreezesOriginalQueryAcrossCallbacksAndUnsafeNames`, `api/find_identity_images_test.go:TestNativeFindIdentityImagesQuerySnapshotsAndConcurrentReuse`, `api/find_identity_images_test.go:TestNativeFindIdentityImagesKeepsLiveProviderHeadersAndCallerContext`, `connection_find_identity_image_test.go:TestConnectionImageFindIdentityHiddenSearch`.
- Explicit Go raw status is preserved as a frozen wire query in both GET and fallback LIST even for statusless native models; nil/empty/repeated values and caller name precedence are retained. Raw status alone does not activate local response filtering, and server query rejection remains a terminal LIST error rather than missing suppression.
  Tests: `api/find_identity_wire_status_test.go:TestNativeFindIdentityWireStatusSurvivesBothPhases`, `api/find_identity_wire_status_test.go:TestNativeFindIdentityWireStatusServerRejectionIsTerminal`.
- FindImageRecord performs a literal escaped ID GET with the owned Image projector; only clean final native400/403/404 enter exact ID-or-name listing. A complete successful normal-list absence triggers a hidden list using the frozen original caller query plus mandatory os_hidden=True and without the auto-name hint. Visible duplicates and normal-list failures never enter hidden lookup.
  Tests: `image/image_record_find_test.go:TestFindImageRecordDefaultHiddenPhases`, `image/image_record_find_test.go:TestFindImageRecordErrorsNeverBecomeHiddenSearch`.
- Actual200..399 GET returns the owned seeded record, including tolerated invalid JSON; accepted malformed shapes/conversions and nonaccepted nonselected errors remain terminal. Default missing returns nil only after both successful empty searches; strict IgnoreMissing=false returns ErrNotFound without a suppressed GET cause.
  Tests: `image/image_record_find_test.go:TestFindImageRecordAcceptedGetAndAbsence`, `image/image_record_find_test.go:TestFindImageRecordErrorsNeverBecomeHiddenSearch`.
- Both fallback searches consume the shared owned list pager, compare exact passive id/name, continue after the first candidate to prove uniqueness, and discard it after duplicates or later page/row/transport failures. Invalid passive row IDs may be compared but never form a new URI. The selected row is not re-fetched.
  Tests: `image/image_record_find_test.go:TestFindImageRecordDefaultHiddenPhases`, `image/image_record_find_test.go:TestFindImageRecordErrorsNeverBecomeHiddenSearch`, `resource/find_identity_response_id_test.go:TestCollectionFindIdentityPassiveIDsAreOnlyCompared`.
- Rejected selected400/403/404 body Read/Close/transport/cancellation/native-hook/source failures are terminal rather than hidden absence. PreserveCleanRetry is applied only to these selected Glance lookup rejections; actual rejected evidence and underlying causes are retained.
  Tests: `image/image_record_find_test.go:TestFindImageRecordRejectedOwnershipFaultsAreTerminal`, `image/image_record_find_test.go:TestFindImageRecordErrorsNeverBecomeHiddenSearch`.
- One prepared source/location snapshot and one concrete option evaluation span GET, normal and hidden pages. Nested List options and filters affect lists only; List.Headers apply to all phases and Find.Headers override them. Option slices/maps/pointers are copied; source checks between nested callbacks stop mutation before HTTP.
  Tests: `image/image_record_find_test.go:TestFindImageRecordOptionsAndLocationOwnAllPhases`, `image/image_record_find_test.go:TestFindImageRecordNestedCallbackSourceChangeStopsBeforeHTTP`.
- Root Connection executes owned literal get, two-page list and hidden find through the same source and current location, retaining independent declared/wire channels and live original auth. Existing native FindIdentity behavior and direct typed Image APIs are preserved as separate profiles.
  Tests: `connection_image_records_test.go:TestConnectionImageRecordsShareSourceLocationAndHiddenDiscovery`, `image/image_record_find_test.go:TestFindImageRecordOptionsAndLocationOwnAllPhases`.

Python/Go differences:

- Go exposes SDK-owned concrete options and additional raw wire query on FindIdentity; the pinned direct find_image declaration (openstack/image/v2/_proxy.py:1013-1033) accepts only name_or_id and ignore_missing. Python query alias is_hidden is not automatically transposed; Go uses os_hidden. details/all_projects are not capabilities of this Image binding.
- Unsafe route strings use literal list-only lookup and invalid identities/result IDs fail before further HTTP. Go keeps the native Image extractor/pager and full-page native decode policy; this does not claim Python descriptor coercion, arbitrary-precision unknown numeric fields or every inherited continuation form.
- Earlier contracts and differences above describe the preserved native/typed API profiles. The additional owned ImageRecord default profile supplies the missing declared Image projector, constructor packing, descriptor conversion, aliases, generic paging and lookup behavior described by the new contracts below; existing strict typed/native behavior is unchanged.
- The added ImageRecord APIs are fresh immutable Go snapshots for the named default profile. They do not mutate a supplied Python-like Resource or implement its dirty state, same-instance fetch, configured Adapter cache/conditional GET, dynamic session/microversion/base-path replacement, or arbitrary Python descriptor objects. These full SDK contracts remain explicitly tracked as SDK-R1, SDK-C1, SDK-J1 and SDK-S1 in docs/implementation-plan.md; API mapping here does not mean complete Python runtime equivalence or live cloud validation.
- FindImageRecord List options are a Go extension; the pinned public find_image signature accepts only name_or_id and ignore_missing. Find uses the owned JSON-domain projector/pager rather than the preserved native Image extractor, allows safe escaped slash/space IDs, and keeps unsafe control/dot strings preflight errors. Arbitrary Python Resource identity and session/cache behavior remain common SDK work.

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordImportMethod`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordImportURI`

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordImportField`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordImportFields`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordImportMethodField`

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image/v2/serviceinfo.StoreImportIdentity`

- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: ImportImage starts with a fresh metadata GET of the explicit ID. ImportKnownImage copies only supplied native ID/container/disk formats before option callbacks and sends no GET. The default request is one glance-direct POST with no empty URI or seeded model fields; incidental response IDs never retarget the requested import ID.
  Tests: `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportFreshAndKnownAcknowledgements`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_core_test.go:TestImportCoreKnownSeedAndHTTPBodySnapshots`.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: An explicit Name uses the existing Images collection exact-name and advertised native paging resolver once, then freshly GETs the resolved ID rather than reusing listed formats. Case decoys, missing names, duplicate matches and errors on later pages terminate before metadata GET or import POST.
  Tests: `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportFreshFormatsAndAllPageNameBinding`.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: Fresh metadata must be a flat UTF-8 object accepted as200, decode the complete native Image shape, and contain canonical lowercase nonempty container_format/disk_format strings. Missing/null/case-only/typed-invalid formats or unrelated malformed native fields/time preserve actual accepted GET body/header/status with ResponseError and never POST.
  Tests: `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportFreshFormatsAndAllPageNameBinding`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_core_test.go:TestImportCoreFreshCanonicalFormatsAndNativeDecodeEvidence`.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: Owned method options default empty Method to glance-direct and preserve unknown nonempty server methods. Web URI is required and prohibited for other methods. Glance-download requires region+image ID and emits optional interface only when present; incomplete/orphan/wrong-method remote values reject before lookup.
  Tests: `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportMethodsAndRootStoreControls`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_options_test.go:TestImportOptionsMethodBodyAndOwnedNamespaces`.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: Singular Store selects only X-Image-Meta-Store while plural Stores selects only the root JSON array. Explicit false root booleans remain present; nil booleans and nil/empty Stores omit. Store plus nonempty Stores or true AllStores conflicts, and explicit empty Store/empty entries reject; an inherited legacy store header conflicts with plural/all-store selection before HTTP.
  Tests: `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportMethodsAndRootStoreControls`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_options_test.go:TestImportOptionsMethodBodyAndOwnedNamespaces`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_core_test.go:TestImportCorePreflightAndSourceBeforeEveryPhase`, `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportExtensionsAndPreflightAreSDKOwned`, `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportLiveProviderFixedRoutesAndSourceRechecks`.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: Root Fields and MethodFields are separate SDK-owned JSON extension namespaces. Snapshot encoding retains nested JSON, null and exact json.Number values; protected core keys and aliases cannot replace method/store/booleans or method name/URI/remote controls. Non-JSON values fail before HTTP.
  Tests: `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportExtensionsAndPreflightAreSDKOwned`, `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportOptionSnapshotsReplacementAndReuse`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_options_test.go:TestImportOptionsMethodBodyAndOwnedNamespaces`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_options_test.go:TestImportOptionsOwnedSnapshotsReplacementAndConcurrentReuse`.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: Ordinary headers are canonicalized with sequential last-wins helpers, frozen and independently reused. Different values under case aliases and protected auth/transport/version/store headers reject. A configured explicit image version header must agree with the selected source Microversion.
  Tests: `image/v2/imageimport/import_options_test.go:TestImportOptionsHeaderAuthorityAndCanonicalLastWins`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_core_test.go:TestImportCorePreflightAndSourceBeforeEveryPhase`, `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportExtensionsAndPreflightAreSDKOwned`, `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportLiveProviderFixedRoutesAndSourceRechecks`.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: WithImportOpts snapshots pointers, Stores slices, header maps and both nested JSON extension maps and replaces the whole options value; individual helpers apply afterward. Bulk empty restores the default method and clears prior controls. Parsed custom configs, supplied KnownImage mutation during options and concurrent reusable options do not alter frozen request data or another response.
  Tests: `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportOptionSnapshotsReplacementAndReuse`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_options_test.go:TestImportOptionsOwnedSnapshotsReplacementAndConcurrentReuse`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_core_test.go:TestImportCoreKnownSeedAndHTTPBodySnapshots`.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: ImportResult reports the fixed ImageID and only actual accepted202 Body/Header/StatusCode. Empty, arbitrary acknowledgement and large-number bytes are passive owned evidence, not an active Image/Task model. A foreign Location is retained without following it or deriving identity.
  Tests: `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportHTTPFailuresAndAcceptedEvidence`, `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportFreshAndKnownAcknowledgements`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_core_test.go:TestImportCoreKnownSeedAndHTTPBodySnapshots`.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: Native HTTP/transport, context and accepted body-read failures stay terminal submission errors without fabricated acknowledgements or semantic resend/cleanup. Accepted202 read failure returns the actual partial result and ResponseError; a concurrently expired context is joined with the original native404 evidence instead of hiding either cause.
  Tests: `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportHTTPFailuresAndAcceptedEvidence`, `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportReadTransportAndCancellationAreTerminal`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_core_test.go:TestImportCoreAcceptedReadFailureAndNativeErrors`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_core_test.go:TestImportCoreCanceledNativeErrorsPreserveBothCauses`.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: The workflow captures client settings/headers and fixes ResourceBase-aware metadata GET/import POST routes while sharing the original Provider. Live token rotation, request context, selected Microversion and configured middleware survive GET/POST; configured401 reauth and503 retry retain the same import body and target. Invalidating source changes after options or GET stop before the next phase, redirects cannot change the image, and explicit Store overlays the configured legacy value without modifying the original client.
  Tests: `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportLiveProviderFixedRoutesAndSourceRechecks`, `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportFreshAndKnownAcknowledgements`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_core_test.go:TestImportCorePreflightAndSourceBeforeEveryPhase`, `image/v2/imageimport/import_core_test.go:TestImportCoreAcceptedReadFailureAndNativeErrors`.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: Existing native Create/Get and error-only Create result, Image aliases and the unsupported specialized inventory row remain separate. Generator gates verify actual native functions/method constants and canonical string Image identity/formats, reject drift or unrelated opt-in, preserve generated native declarations byte-for-byte and do not invent an import resource collection.
  Tests: `api/glance_import_contracts_test.go:TestGlanceImportNativeCreateGetAndImageModelsStayUnchanged`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_import_test.go:TestGlanceImportKeepsActualNativeCallsAndNoInventedCollection`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_import_test.go:TestGlanceImportRejectsNativeIdentityFormatsAndConstantsDrift`.
- Select literal ID or SDK-produced Record private current ID once; escape once and ignore public Resource/Wire/Envelope/Location for routing. Source _get_resource performs no GET/list/find. ID-only Image has no formats and fails locally before POST.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportPreflightRejectsUnsafeSelectorsDescriptorsJSONAndProtectedNamespaces`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportOpaqueAcceptedResponsesKeepPreparedRecordAndIndependentReceipt`, `image_record_import_integration_test.go:TestConnectionOwnedImageImportRejectsInvalidInvocation`.
- Existing Image _get_resource._update({}) preserves full current/original/dirty/presence, refreshes current location, resets plain image_import_methods and materializes every declared descriptor before HTTP. Prior fetch receipts and private borrowed data stay unchanged; immutable returned record does not alias caller.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportOpaqueAcceptedResponsesKeepPreparedRecordAndIndependentReceipt`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportAcknowledgementDoesNotCleanPendingBodyOrDiscardBorrowedData`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportCapturesPrivateImageStoreOptionsAndLocationBeforeCallbacks`.
- Unrelated descriptor projection may fail before import HTTP. container_format and disk_format are untyped Body values, requiring Python truthiness of both private current values rather than canonical nonempty strings. Any truthy complete JSON is allowed; missing/null/false/0/empty-string/list/object fail. SDK Source adds no local method status or format allow-list check.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportFormatsUseRawPythonTruthinessWithoutStatusOrAllowList`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportPreflightRejectsUnsafeSelectorsDescriptorsJSONAndProtectedNamespaces`.
- Method default glance-direct applies only when omitted. Explicit None/null, empty/unknown string, boolean, number, list or object pass through as method.name; Source does not runtime-enforce its str annotation. Truthy URI still requires equality to exact string web-download. Preserve nil-omitted versus explicit raw-null via owned JSON presence, never blanket empty-to-default or non-string validation.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportMethodPreservesOmittedNullUnknownAndAdminBranchValues`.
- Any Python-truthy JSON URI value requires method equal to exact string web-download and enters method.uri unchanged. None/null/false/0/empty-string/list/object URI is omitted under every method. web-download without URI is submitted. Source annotations do not enforce URI string at runtime; legacy required-URI or Go string-only forms are separate profiles.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportURIUsesTruthyDomainAndExactWebDownloadGuard`.
- Remote region+image ID+service interface enter all3 method.glance_* fields iff all3 values are Python-truthy, independent of method. Complete non-string JSON triples (bool/number/list/object) enter unchanged; incomplete/None/falsey tuples silently omit. SDK Source does not runtime-enforce annotations, require a tuple for glance-download, or reject complete remote values under another method.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportRemoteTupleRequiresAllThreeTruthyValuesUnderAnyMethod`.
- Singular store and each plural store convert locally without GET/list/discovery. None singular omits; each plural None constructs bare Store with ID None. String/numeric/bool/list literals become id values; JSON dict/Munch constructs Store from its attributes and consumes id, with missing id default None and no name fallback. Store Resource uses private canonical raw id. Owned StoreRecord selection retains raw JSON ID plus missing/null semantics before callbacks, never public Resource/Wire/Envelope identity. Source dynamic-class bounds remain explicit.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportCapturesPrivateImageStoreOptionsAndLocationBeforeCallbacks`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportPluralStoreConstructorsPreserveRawIDsOrderDuplicatesAndMissingNull`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/store_import_identity_test.go:TestStoreRecordImportIdentityRetainsPrivateIDAndReturnsOwnedCopies`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/store_import_identity_test.go:TestStoreRecordImportIdentityMissingAndForgedInputs`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/store_import_identity_test.go:TestStoreImportIdentityConstructorValuesAndOwnership`.
- Truthy all_stores conflicts with singular or nonempty plural stores; explicit false does not. Singular non-None store conflicts with nonempty plural stores. Empty/nil plural is no stores. Source checks/converts in Proxy and repeats store conflicts in Image; Go preserves selection presence and snapshots it.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportFlagsPreserveFalseyPresenceAndRejectTruthyStoreConflicts`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportStoreSelectorsRejectConflictsUnsafeHeadersAndConstructorControls`.
- Singular store emits BOTH root stores:[same canonical raw ID] and X-Image-Meta-Store; empty string is preserved in array and empty header. Plural raw JSON IDs preserve order/duplicates and null/bool/number/list/object values, body only; empty stores sequence omits key. Source bodies do not validate the raw singular header value. Go singular non-string/null rejection is an explicit header-representation boundary, without claiming an equivalent Source None-header/session error.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportSingularStoreEmitsBodyAndCompatibilityHeaderAndRemovesConfiguredDecoys`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportPluralStoreConstructorsPreserveRawIDsOrderDuplicatesAndMissingNull`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportStoreSelectorsRejectConflictsUnsafeHeadersAndConstructorControls`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/store_import_identity_test.go:TestStoreImportIdentityConstructorValuesAndOwnership`.
- Root all_stores and all_stores_must_succeed preserve every non-None JSON value, including false, zero, empty string/list/object or other nonbool values; Source SDK never runtime-enforces bool annotations. all_stores conflict uses Python truthiness. Omitted/None fields stay absent; server owns default false/true and rejects nonbool. Typed bool helpers can coexist with raw JSON opts; backend success/cleanup remain server-owned.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportFlagsPreserveFalseyPresenceAndRejectTruthyStoreConflicts`.
- Concrete With options own pointers/maps/slices/store selectors, full-config replacement and ordinary-header merge. Compiled method/root namespaces cannot be overwritten by optional extension fields. These concrete JSON/option policies are Go additions, without caller builders.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportExtensionAndFullOptionsFactoriesAreSnapshotsAndReusable`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportCapturesPrivateImageStoreOptionsAndLocationBeforeCallbacks`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportPreflightRejectsUnsafeSelectorsDescriptorsJSONAndProtectedNamespaces`.
- Capture original Service/API/Images/provider/source/type/origin/microversion, ordinary source headers, option callbacks and current location once with sticky ancestor/context guards. SDK owns auth/framing/version/representation/store namespaces. Configured X-Image-Meta-Store is removed from captured child only. Shared RequestHeaderPolicy validates selected store-header value or explicit absence at physical dispatch/retry so native callbacks cannot add, remove or change store selection; ordinary native retry headers retain their existing policy. Store conversion remains local JSON without provider binding.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportCapturesPrivateImageStoreOptionsAndLocationBeforeCallbacks`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportOptionAndNestedMarshalerGuardsStopLaterWorkWithCauses`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportSingularStoreEmitsBodyAndCompatibilityHeaderAndRemovesConfiguredDecoys`.
- Submit one logical POST /images/{private ID}/import with frozen JSON/route/store-header policy. Native clean retry/reauth may resend exactly that request with live original provider token, while sticky source/ancestor/context and selected-value/absent store-header guards stop changed scope before physical resend. No lookup/discovery/stage/task/fetch/wait/checksum/cleanup/follow-Location is inserted; custom RoundTripper internal replay remains an explicit transport bound.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportNativeRetryFreezesBodyTargetStoreHeadersAndKeepsLiveAuthentication`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportSafeReauthenticationRetriesOnlyCapturedJSONSubmission`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportOptionAndNestedMarshalerGuardsStopLaterWorkWithCauses`.
- Image.import_image returns raw response without translate: opaque ACK does not parse/overlay/clean Body, read import-method headers, change image status or previous fetch receipts. Prepared Record keeps pending state and empty ImportMethods. Independent Acknowledgement preserves actual Body/Header/Status even invalid JSON/UTF-8.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportOpaqueAcceptedResponsesKeepPreparedRecordAndIndependentReceipt`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportAcknowledgementDoesNotCleanPendingBodyOrDiscardBorrowedData`.
- Source Proxy.request defaults raise_exc=False and Import never calls raise_from_response, allowing raw400..599. Owned Go broad200399 plus explicit native400599 errors are documented policy. Rejection has no accepted ACK; accepted Read/Close/source/context failures retain only actual partial ACK and inspectable causes, without synthetic completion/recovery.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportAcceptedPhysicalFailuresRetainOnlyActualPartialAcknowledgement`, `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportNativeRejectionsPreserveErrorEvidenceAndNeverAcknowledge`.
- glance-direct/web-download/glance-download and exact copy-image are mandatory same-public-function branches plus shared Connection facade. Glance exact copy_image policy is project-admin; ordinary modify_image is admin-or-project-member/owner. Server policy/quota/status/store/task/backend and SDK-R1/C1/S1 remain explicit; legacy API/tests/native ABI/catalog stay unchanged.
  Tests: `image/image_record_import_test.go:TestImageRecordImportMethodPreservesOmittedNullUnknownAndAdminBranchValues`, `image_record_import_integration_test.go:TestConnectionOwnedImageImportSharesSourceAndPreservesBorrowedData`, `image_record_import_integration_test.go:TestConnectionOwnedImageImportRejectsInvalidInvocation`.

Python/Go differences:

- Principal public graph: Source _get_resource converts supplied Image locally and projects current descriptors/location/import-method reset, validates truthy container/disk formats, converts singular/plural Store inputs, builds one interoperable import JSON request and returns a raw asynchronous POST response. Owned ImportImageRecord implements that complete finite graph with immutable SDK-produced private JSON state and concrete SDK-owned selectors. ID-only seed has no formats and fails locally; callers explicitly fetch first. Native/ref ImportImage and ImportKnownImage remain separate legacy strict202 profiles with existing API/test references retained and scoped.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: Python _get_resource constructs or accepts mutable Image/Store Resources without a GET, and the proxy requires supplied container/disk formats. Go ImportImage(Ref) freshly fetches metadata, resolving an explicit Name once through existing exact-name collection policy; ImportKnownImage copies only native ID/container/disk strings and does not fetch.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: The Go known-image/native shape does not reproduce arbitrary supplied Resource subclasses, dict/Munch construction, dirty state or cached descriptor defaults. Fresh GET performs native whole-image decoding and requires canonical lowercase nonempty format strings with valid UTF-8; the requested ID stays fixed even if response ID differs.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: Python singular Store sends both a stores array and compatibility header. Go Store is header-only and plural Stores is body-only, rejects configured store-header conflicts with plural/all-store requests, and permits an explicit typed Store to replace the configured legacy value.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: Go requires web-download URI and glance-download remote region+image ID, permits optional omitted interface for the server default, and rejects orphan/wrong-method remote values. Python can send web without URI or silently omit an incomplete remote tuple. Unknown nonempty methods remain server-owned.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: Concrete SDK options and JSON extensions snapshot owned request data, enforce namespace/header/source boundaries, and expose original Provider transport configuration rather than arbitrary caller native builders or injected Python sessions. Cloud custom extension support remains server-owned.
- Legacy native/ref strict202 import profile only: Go returns an actual202 ImportResult acknowledgement with partial accepted-read evidence, rather than the raw Python requests.Response. It does not imply completed storage, return a synthetic active Image/Task or automatically stage, discover, wait, clean up or follow Location.
- Source mutates the same arbitrary Image/Store Resource, dict/Munch or subclass and dynamically reads body/descriptor/current-location state. Go snapshots full private current/original/dirty/presence and borrowed data, projects a captured location, resets plain ImportMethods before dispatch and preserves earlier passive fetch receipts. Imported response is not translated, overlaid or used to clean Body; independent actual ACK and immutable Record expose different ownership/lifecycle. Source dynamic class/constructor/object domains remain SDK-R1.
- Owned JSON Method distinguishes omitted default glance-direct from explicit null/empty/non-string/unknown values. URI uses Python truthiness and exact web-download equality; complete truthy remote region/image/interface values emit together independent of method, while incomplete tuples omit. Non-None root all_stores/must_succeed raw values preserve presence, including false and nonbool JSON for server validation. Store selectors consume only canonical Attributes id, use null for missing identity, preserve all plural JSON IDs/order/duplicates, and singular empty string enters both stores array and compatibility header. Concrete typed With helpers coexist with owned raw JSON; legacy stricter URI/remote/empty-method/singular-header-only behavior is unchanged.
- Source Image.import_image never calls _translate_response or raise_from_response and Proxy.request defaults raise_exc=False, so arbitrary adapters can return raw400..599 without an exception. The owned Go profile deliberately uses native400..599 errors and accepts actual200..399 opaque acknowledgements; malformed JSON/UTF-8 bytes stay passive. Accepted physical/source/context failures return actual partial ACK with inspectable causes and no fabricated completed Record or recovery. Singular non-string/null store ID fails the Go HTTP-header representation check before POST; no equivalent Source None-header/session exception is claimed.
- The SDK owns concrete JSON/header snapshots, fixed private image identity, source/provider/origin/microversion and sticky outer/context guards before one logical POST. Shared RequestHeaderPolicy protects the selected store compatibility header value or its absence at physical dispatch and native retry; callbacks cannot change store selection while ordinary native headers retain configured policy. Clean native JSON auth/retry keeps captured body/route with live original provider token; guard drift prevents resend. No initial lookup, method discovery, stage, task, final fetch, wait, checksum, cleanup or follow-Location is inserted. Full adapter cache/invalidation/metrics/global request configuration and arbitrary session/response policy remain SDK-C1/S1; custom RoundTripper internal behavior is outside the logical-request guarantee. Raw JSON precision, strict UTF-8/paired-surrogate validation and constructor/header namespaces are explicit Go representation policies, without arbitrary Python object or byte-for-byte serializer equivalence.
- Pinned Glance selects exact copy-image through project-admin copy_image, and other methods through modify_image admin-or-project-member/owner. This mandatory admin method shares the same public Import signature; ordinary glance-direct/web-download/glance-download remain core user priority. Image state/format/UUID/URI, deployed import methods/stores, quota, lock/proxy-worker and asynchronous backend task outcome stay server-owned. This finite owned go_mapping does not promote public create_image, upload_image or download_image or establish authenticated cloud/storage completion.

- CreateAndImport defaults to qcow2/bare/private and glance-direct with waiting disabled. Direct execution performs actual creation201, staging204, post-stage metadata200 and import202 without an initial image lookup, duplicate/discovery request or synthetic active result. Phase responses and fixed ImageID are retained separately.
  Tests: `image/create_import_contracts_test.go:TestCreateAndImportDefaultDirectSequenceAndEvidence`, `image/create_import_core_test.go:TestCreateImportFixedCanonicalRouteAndIndependentActualEvidence`.
- Concrete metadata, stage, import and wait policies, source/reference validity, reader requirements and protected namespaces are checked before the first create POST or input read. Typed nil, invalid source/context, unknown fresh workflow method, conflicting selector and invalid field/header/size/wait policies stop locally.
  Tests: `image/create_import_contracts_test.go:TestCreateAndImportCompletePreflight`, `image/create_import_options_test.go:TestCreateImportWaitOptionsHaveConcretePreflightPolicy`, `image/create_import_core_test.go:TestCreateImportPreflightValidatesEveryPhaseBeforeCreationOrReading`.
- PrepareStageOptions validates the selected source, applies a caller callback once and returns independently owned concrete size/header settings without HTTP or reader use. Retained callback configuration cannot later alter the prepared snapshot; invalid configured source headers are rejected before callbacks.
  Tests: `image/v2/imagedata/stage_prepare_test.go:TestPrepareStageOptionsFreezesOnceWithoutRequests`.
- PrepareImportOptions is a local-only source-aware snapshot seam. It preserves owned JSON extension numbers and store slices after one callback application, rejects conflicting configured store headers and selected plural/all-store controls, and permits an explicit singular-store overlay without changing the shared source.
  Tests: `image/v2/imageimport/import_prepare_test.go:TestPrepareImportOptionsFreezesJSONAndSourceConflictsWithoutRequests`.
- The original lowercase creation id must be a nonempty safe route segment and becomes the fixed workflow ImageID. Typed case aliases, foreign Location and later response IDs do not retarget staging, import or wait. Direct creation also requires exact canonical queued status before consuming Data.
  Tests: `image/create_import_contracts_test.go:TestCreateAndImportCanonicalIdentityAndPostStageFormats`, `image/create_import_contracts_test.go:TestCreateAndImportDefaultDirectSequenceAndEvidence`, `image/create_import_core_test.go:TestCreateImportFixedCanonicalRouteAndIndependentActualEvidence`.
- Direct import reads required lowercase container_format and disk_format from the actual post-stage metadata body, independently of returned typed case aliases or stale creation formats. Missing/null/empty canonical decision fields preserve the actual accepted response and stop before import.
  Tests: `image/create_import_contracts_test.go:TestCreateAndImportCanonicalIdentityAndPostStageFormats`, `image/create_import_core_test.go:TestCreateImportCanonicalFailureRetainsAcceptedCreationAndStage`.
- The workflow metadata policy reuses the existing upload builder, retaining optional false and explicit min_disk/min_ram zero and root JSON extensions without replacing protected core fields. Full and namespace helpers snapshot pointers, slices, maps and JSON. Deferred leaf callback batches run once during preparation and can be cleared by later concrete helpers; reusable policies remain independent. Invalid custom JSON retains the original MarshalerError and caller cause alongside ErrInvalidOption.
  Tests: `image/create_import_contracts_test.go:TestCreateAndImportPreparedSnapshotsAndCallbacksOnce`, `image/create_import_options_test.go:TestCreateImportOptionsOwnReplacementAndReusableSnapshots`, `image/create_import_options_test.go:TestCreateImportDeferredPhaseCallbacksAreAppliedOnceAndCanBeCleared`, `image/create_import_options_test.go:TestCreateImportMetadataReusesUploadBuilderAndPreservesZeroFields`, `image/create_import_options_test.go:TestCreateImportMetadataRetainsCustomJSONFailureCause`.
- Fresh web-download and glance-download workflows create metadata and submit import with no reader or staging phase. Required URI/remote inputs and unused stage controls are guarded before creation. Remote formats come from the actual creation response, plural stores retain repeats and explicit false flags, and singular Store remains header-only. Copy-image and unknown fresh methods are unsupported while the existing leaf passthrough remains separate.
  Tests: `image/create_import_contracts_test.go:TestCreateAndImportRemoteModesAndSelectors`, `image/create_import_contracts_test.go:TestCreateAndImportCompletePreflight`.
- Direct data is borrowed at its current cursor without input Close/Seek/size inspection/buffering or semantic replay. Stage headers and metadata POST-only headers are kept in their selected phases, while size is header-only and explicit zero can be retained. The binary provider replay policy stays distinct from configured JSON requests.

Later stage/read/followup/import failures preserve prior actual201/204/200/202 proof and original errors without automatic delete, recreation, recovery GET or fabricated model. Response bodies/headers and models from different phases are independently owned.

- Optional active waiting uses concrete SDK-owned policy: nil Wait disables, selected nil timeout/interval use five minutes/two seconds, zero timeout removes the SDK deadline, nil failures select killed/deleted and nonnil empty failures disable them. The parent context bounds all phases; only an actual successful wait populates Ready and prior accepted phase proof remains on failure.

- Advertised glance-direct, web-download, glance-download, copy-image and custom methods use the complete owned raw Import compiler without image/store/method discovery. Borrowed lifetimes and actual accepted phase receipts remain independent and no fabricated ready/active/proof is returned. - Preserve all12 legacy CreateAndImport contract objects/test refs and its explicit fresh-image/queued/post-stage/default/wait/no-cleanup profile; add the complete modern whole Service and Connection creation graph separately. Public whole-project/service guides and executable standalone/Connection examples must cover all modern routes and explicit differences. Promotion is allowed only after actual focused/full checks, generator zero drift, local/remote consumers and Source/contract/test inventories are verified; global SDK-R1/C1/S1 and standalone Swift/Task rows remain unresolved.
  Tests: `image_record_create_integration_test.go:TestConnectionOwnedImageCreateComposesPolicyAndKeepsSwiftLazy`, `image_record_create_integration_test.go:TestConnectionOwnedImageCreateRejectsInvalidInvocation`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_create_import_test.go:TestGlanceCreateImportKeepsNativeImageAPIAndDocumentsUpperService`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_create_import_test.go:TestGlanceCreateImportRejectsNativeMetadataAndResponseFieldDrift`.

Python/Go differences:

- Legacy CreateAndImport profile: CreateAndImport is an explicit fresh-image import workflow with qcow2/bare/private, glance-direct and no-wait defaults. Python create_image additionally selects metadata-only/direct-file/configured Swift task paths and uses cloud format/vendor/container configuration.
- Legacy CreateAndImport profile: Go prepares and freezes concrete metadata/stage/import/wait policy before POST or reader consumption, using local-only SDK seams and separate callback namespaces. Python kwargs/meta conversion, descriptor values and mutable Resource/session behavior are broader.
- Legacy CreateAndImport profile: Python non-task _upload_image_put does not receive create_image wait or timeout. Go optional active wait is an explicit five-minute/two-second policy, with caller overrides and parent context, rather than a reproduction of timeout3600 for that source branch.
- Legacy CreateAndImport profile: Go preserves actual accepted201/204/200/202 phase evidence and performs no cleanup DELETE after failure. Python checks response import-method capability after creation, stages without this post-stage metadata GET and deletes upload/import/checksum failures inside its try block.
- Legacy CreateAndImport profile: Go canonical created ID and original lowercase post-stage format decisions reject missing/unsafe data while typed case aliases and passive Location cannot choose the route. Go retains native typed projection/header behavior alongside independent raw response evidence.
- Legacy CreateAndImport profile: Go explicit plural stores and optional false flags are preserved together, while Python stores-is-not-None suppresses both all-store flags. Existing leaf singular Store is header-only rather than Python header plus one-element stores body.
- Legacy CreateAndImport profile: Go borrows explicit io.Reader without filename/name inference, hashing, automatic whole-file size or checksum validation. Binary staging disables SDK replay callbacks and redirects; caller RoundTripper internals and blocking Read release remain caller responsibilities.
- Modern whole CreateImageRecord covers the complete finite JSON/source-fixed create_image graph. Legacy CreateAndImport remains a separately named stricter fresh-image import profile with its preserved12 contracts; its defaults/queued/followupGET/active-wait/no-cleanup differences do not describe modern creation.
- Go owns immutable tagged data, raw JSON/maps/headers/store/config snapshots and independent Image/Task/phase/cleanup receipts, rather than mutating arbitrary Python Resource/Munch/subclasses. Raw Body/current/original/dirty presence, constructor hook ordering, Source-style captured current location and header collector reset reuse owned Resource helpers; full dynamic class/component/default alias/header/URI/backing lifecycle remains SDK-R1.
- Go native400..599 import rejection and retained response causes are explicit improvements over Source returned no-raise import ACK. Errors within selected binary/import/checksum block delete current private image ID; no successful import is equated with backend completion or active status.
- Go closes SDK-owned filenames once and clears their retained data reference; Source opens its upload file without a comparable owned closure. Borrowed Readers stay open/current-cursor, binary replay disabled, and blocked caller Read or custom RoundTripper internal replay still requires caller cooperation.
- Go joins original and cleanup faults rather than Source masking or cloud SDKException suppression, preserves actual partial receipts and keeps source/context failure sticky. Cleanup uses the same captured context/source and is never detached to bypass a failed binding.
- Source signed arbitrary Python integer/bool Size is retained in the JSON domain, including bool True/False; float/string/container size rejects inside cleanup. Size inference is restricted to Source file-like filename/Reader capability, excluding artificial Go Bytes/Text seekability. Task timeout null/zero/bool/number/fraction is retained but positive timer values beyond Go duration range are saturated and tiny positives bounded to1ns.
- Swift filename composition reuses unique segment prefixes, immutable replay sections, exact-owned cleanup and ambiguity policies instead of reproducing the Source FileSegment cursor bug. Image-only profile broad200..399 and segment202 differ from unchanged standalone Swift gates. Arbitrary valid UTF-8 string hashes are accepted without hex restrictions; nonstring filename hashes lie outside the concrete Swift string bridge, while raw-data hashes are ignored like Source.
- The actual pinned filename-extension inference typo is preserved. No corrected inference mode or extra default/private/queued/active gates are introduced.
- Only clean disabled/object-store absence becomes unavailable; mixed/auth/IO/source errors propagate rather than broad Source has_service suppression. Formal Go request fields and options are separate concrete namespaces where Python duplicate-argument binding cannot be represented; fixed string-key JSON, complete UTF-8/paired-surrogate routing, safe literal IDs/header/source controls and JSON serializer order remain explicit bounds.
- Native server policy including own-project modify/community visibility and admin public/other-owner/copy-image/tasks access, store availability, Swift ACL/quota/URI authorization and eventual backend processing remain server-owned. Local tests cannot establish live cloud success or universal policy overrides.
- Captured provider/origin/base/type/microversion and source-only nested guards preserve current operation. Full Adapter caching/conditional GET/invalidation/metrics/global request configuration remain SDK-C1; arbitrary session/adapter/response/microversion/base-path injection remains SDK-S1. The global shared objectives and standalone Swift/Task declaration rows are not promoted by this finite modern operation.
- Request-owned Task/HTTP timer expiry does not invalidate live parent-source invariants; caller cancellation or real binding/source drift, including joined timeout errors, stays sticky and cannot be bypassed by detached cleanup. Ordinary response closure can cancel native request timers without manufacturing failed Swift uploads.
- The modern import phase reuses the owned ImportImageRecord policy: configured legacy store headers are discarded from the copied request client, then an explicit singular selection supplies its fixed header. Source/shared header maps stay unchanged.

### `python:image/v2/download_image`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.Service.DownloadTo`

- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: DownloadTo defaults to metadata GET200 then full binary GET200, using a bounded 1MiB copy chunk and available checksum verification. It retains independent actual native Image, raw metadata body/header/status, actual accepted binary header/status and exact BytesWritten without keeping the full payload or fabricating completion.
  Tests: `image/download_contracts_test.go:TestDownloadToDefaultSequenceAndEvidence`, `image/download_core_test.go:TestDownloadCoreFixedRouteBoundedCopyAndOwnedEvidence`.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: Service/source/provider/context/ref/writer and concrete options are validated before any HTTP or Write. Nil and typed-nil writers, invalid route/source/header/Range/If-Range controls, nil callbacks, nonpositive or over64MiB chunks and malformed store preferences stop locally. Nil chunk selects1MiB and nil verification selects true; explicit false remains distinct.
  Tests: `image/download_contracts_test.go:TestDownloadToCompletePreflight`, `image/download_options_test.go:TestDownloadOptionsDefaultsAndBoundedPreflight`.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: Whole-policy and leaf helpers snapshot pointers and preferences for last replacement, independent reuse and concurrent application. Custom callbacks run once before requests and their retained configuration cannot alter transfer options. Preferences preserve order and duplicates in one URL-encoded comma-separated prefer value; nil/empty omit it.
  Tests: `image/download_contracts_test.go:TestDownloadToPreparedSnapshotsAndPreferences`, `image/download_options_test.go:TestDownloadOptionsOwnFullReplacementAndConcurrentReuse`, `image/download_options_test.go:TestDownloadOptionsCustomCallbacksOnceAndOwnedPointers`.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: Explicit ID chooses one fresh metadata GET; Name uses existing exact-name full-page resolution followed by fresh GET. The exact lowercase canonical metadata id must equal the fixed chosen ID. Incidental typed case aliases and response self/file/schema/Location cannot choose the binary route. Actual native projection remains separate from original response evidence; Name pager continuation keeps existing native semantics.
  Tests: `image/download_contracts_test.go:TestDownloadToCanonicalIdentityAndIntegrity`, `image/download_core_test.go:TestDownloadCoreRetainsAcceptedMetadataAndStopsInvalidIdentity`, `image/download_core_test.go:TestDownloadCoreFixedRouteBoundedCopyAndOwnedEvidence`.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: Canonical complete nonempty os_hash_algo/os_hash_value wins over legacy metadata checksum, which wins over literal binary Content-MD5. Incomplete pairs fall back; disabled verification skips canonical hash-field validation while retaining native Image decode. The bounded algorithms md5/sha1/sha224/sha256/sha384/sha512/sha512_224/sha512_256 are implemented. Unsupported primary algorithms and malformed canonical hash types fail before binary GET. Expected strings are not hex-normalized or base64-decoded.
  Tests: `image/download_contracts_test.go:TestDownloadToCanonicalIdentityAndIntegrity`, `image/download_core_test.go:TestDownloadCoreCanonicalHashSelectionAndAlgorithms`, `image/download_core_test.go:TestDownloadCoreNoDataRejectedPartialAndLiteralHashMismatch`.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: Only actual binary200/204 are accepted. Actual204 performs no Write, clears checksum evidence and retains header/status. Unsolicited206 or HTTP errors retain metadata plus original native rejection without an accepted binary status claim. Metadata-selected algorithm/expected survive binary open/rejection failures. Nonempty Content-Range on actual200 is rejected before Write with accepted200/header and selected metadata proof retained.
  Tests: `image/download_contracts_test.go:TestDownloadToNoDataAndNativeCompatibility`, `image/download_core_test.go:TestDownloadCoreNoDataRejectedPartialAndLiteralHashMismatch`.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: Before accepted metadata200 the result is nil. Accepted metadata read/decode/canonical/native-model failures retain raw200 body/header/status and original error, stop before binary GET and do not Write. Later file/open/read/write/short-write failures retain prior metadata, any actual accepted binary response and exact writer-reported successful bytes without rollback, body replay or recovery requests.
  Tests: `image/download_contracts_test.go:TestDownloadToPartialTransferFailures`, `image/download_core_test.go:TestDownloadCoreRetainsAcceptedMetadataAndStopsInvalidIdentity`.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: The borrowed writer is never closed, sought or truncated. The owned chunk loop does not use ReaderFrom/WriterTo shortcuts, enforces short writes and counts reported written bytes. Simultaneous read/write errors, accepted-body Close errors and context/custom causes remain inspectable. SDK accepted bodies close once and native Request owns rejected bodies. Actual/Complete require full EOF and successful writes; Verified requires a matching digest. Typed DownloadChecksumMismatchError unwraps ErrChecksumMismatch and carries algorithm/expected/actual. Close-only failure after valid full transfer preserves Complete/Verified proof; partial failures do not claim them.
  Tests: `image/download_contracts_test.go:TestDownloadToPartialTransferFailures`, `image/download_contracts_test.go:TestDownloadToBorrowedWriterCancellationAndClose`, `image/download_core_test.go:TestDownloadCoreJoinsReadWriteCloseAndPreservesFullDigestProof`, `image/download_core_test.go:TestDownloadCoreNoDataRejectedPartialAndLiteralHashMismatch`.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: Captured prefix/headers/client selection use the original provider live token and configured pre-body reauthentication/retry. Original source/provider changes are checked before subsequent requests. Binary redirects are disabled and no accepted-body read/write failure restarts download. Parent deadlines, HTTPClient timeout, cancellation and custom cause remain in returned failures; tests release borrowed blocking writers and close all handles. Name pagination retains the existing pager contract rather than adding a new continuation policy.
  Tests: `image/download_contracts_test.go:TestDownloadToProviderSourceAndRetryBoundaries`, `image/download_contracts_test.go:TestDownloadToBorrowedWriterCancellationAndClose`.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: Existing generated ImageData.Download raw-body API stays independently callable with native defaultGET200 behavior and without workflow metadata or integrity. Generator checks the native Download context/client/id/result signature and Image canonical ID/checksum string fields and rejects type/tag/arity drift. Upper writer documentation does not invent a native operation, model or resource collection; native declarations and inventory remain unchanged.

Python/Go differences:

- Complete finite owned Proxy.download_image graph: literal/private Record/synchronized raw constructor, whole64 raw Body/65 descriptor view, mandatory seeded metadata fetch with sparse overlay/invalid-JSON tolerance and post-fetch identity, after-GET raw checksum selection/fallback and all borrowed-writer/owned-path/default-memory/caller-stream branches. This go_mapping is not full SDK supported parity.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: DownloadTo is an explicit bounded writer workflow with a fresh metadata request and optional available-checksum validation. Python accepts mutable Image or ID, output path/IOBase and stream/no-output modes, with a default whole-memory response branch. Existing raw native Download remains independently available.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: Go fixes the selected ID and requires matching lowercase canonical metadata id, using raw decision fields alongside independent native/raw evidence. Python metadata fetch mutates its supplied Resource and later uses self.id; complete cache/descriptors/session/adapter identity semantics are broader.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: Go concrete options freeze caller pointers/slices and callback results before requests, bound chunk size to1..64MiB, preserve ordered repeated preferences and use URL encoding. Python defaults to1MiB but does not impose this bound or Go snapshot policy; both nil/empty preferences omit prefer.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: Go supports a bounded standard-library hash registry and rejects an unsupported complete primary pair before binary GET. Python performs the binary GET first and commonly falls through to MD5 or unverified transfer when the caught unsupported-hash error includes the algorithm name. Go disabled verification also skips canonical hash-field checks.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: Go exposes selected algorithm/expected and full-EOF Actual/Complete/Verified without changing raw headers, retaining selected metadata hashes on binary failure and full digest proof on Close-only error. Python verifies consumed output after iteration, may rewrite Content-MD5 in raw-stream mode and logs missing hashes instead of returning explicit verification availability.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: Go accepts full200/no-data204, rejects unsolicited206 and other statuses, and rejects Content-Range on accepted200 before writing. Pinned Python binary DownloadMixin does not translate response errors, while Proxy.request defaults raise_exc=False, so returned error bodies may be consumed. These are intentionally stronger Go accepted-status and whole-image policies.
- Legacy typed writer-only DownloadTo profile only: Go enforces short-write counts, retains exact partial bytes and joins read/write/Close/context causes including a typed checksum mismatch. Python output ignores write counts and wraps output errors as text; both can leave already-written partial output. Go closes its HTTP body and borrows the writer without path opening or output rollback.
- Go owns immutable JSON-domain Records/options/raw phase receipts and deferred stream guards. Mutable Python class/constructor extensions R1, optional API cache C1 and full Adapter/discovery/session S1 remain unresolved globally. The separate cloud exact-one-output/name wrapper is not closed by this lower operation.
- Go accepts actual200..399 including204/206 without Content-Range rejection and reports native>=400 errors. Pinned binary DownloadMixin has no status translation and may consume/return error bodies. Accepted204 still verifies its empty payload; binary never translates Record or performs a final metadata fetch.
- Go default registry covers MD5/SHA1/SHA2/truncations/SHA3/BLAKE2/SHAKE and a finite case/hyphen alias policy, not every installed OpenSSL hash. Optional primary-only function factory runs after accepted GET; Unsupported sentinel selects separate SDK md5.New fallback, other errors stop. Raw truthy nonstring expectations mismatch after complete output; selected SHAKE consumed mode reports missing output length after EOF.
- Go retains actual binary Header separately from raw ContentMD5 and PythonString CompatibilityHeader. Only stream-only selected md5 compatibility mutates that clone, preserving Source exact algorithm-name comparison; caller stream is unconsumed and never automatically verified. Typed mismatch, successful byte counts and joined read/write/Close/context/source causes are explicit improvements.
- Go writer/path modes always use a bounded1MiB default copy loop rather than Source requests default stream=False buffering; chunk1..64MiB, fixed literal Unicode identity and source/control guards are explicit bounds. Whole-memory default has no cap. Filename opens/truncates only after metadata/file GET/hash selection, owns close and leaves partial data; borrowed output never closes/seeks/flushes.
- Ordered UTF-8 store preference strings preserve repeats/empty/comma/control values and encode one prefer query instead of Source literal comma query. Backend validation/stable location fallback/cache effects and admin deactivated-image/download policy remain server-owned; static pinned evidence does not prove deployed policy or storage.
- Legacy typed DownloadTo and generated native Download remain separate preserved profiles. Modern public create_image12 and Source dynamic R1/C1/S1 remain unresolved; Upload30/Stage28/Import28 reviewed objects are unchanged.
