- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: DeleteImage defaults to whole-image DELETE and ignores clean missing resources. ID routes directly without metadata GET; nonnil store selects only /stores/{storeID}/{imageID}. Only actual204 constructs a result with fixed selected ImageID/StoreID, owned raw Body, cloned Header and actual StatusCode; no decoded model or fabricated response.
  Tests: `image/delete_contracts_test.go:TestDeleteImageWholeAndStoreRoutesAndEvidence`, `image/delete_core_test.go:TestDeleteCoreFixedScopeAndOwnedAcknowledgement`.
- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: Context/service/provider/image base/headers/ref and all options are validated before HTTP. Nil policy defaults StoreID=nil and IgnoreMissing=true; explicit false remains distinct. Explicit empty/unsafe/invalid UTF8 store fails locally instead of broadening to whole delete. Nil callbacks and caller callback failures preserve the invalid-option or original cause.
  Tests: `image/delete_contracts_test.go:TestDeleteImageCompletePreflight`, `image/delete_options_test.go:TestDeleteOptionsDefaultsAndExplicitStorePreflight`.
- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: Whole-policy helper snapshots both pointers at construction and fully replaces prior settings; leaf helpers replace one setting in order. Option slice and each callback configuration are owned. Custom callbacks run once before requests, and retained configuration cannot mutate later callbacks or the final policy. Reused snapshot helpers remain independent under concurrent application.
  Tests: `image/delete_contracts_test.go:TestDeleteImagePreparedSnapshotsAndSource`, `image/delete_options_test.go:TestDeleteOptionsFullReplacementAndConcurrentReuse`, `image/delete_options_test.go:TestDeleteOptionsCallbacksOnceAndIndependentRetainedHandles`.
- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: Explicit Name uses existing exact-name full-page Images resolver and duplicate detection, then a fixed safe DELETE ID. Only direct logical image NotFound from completed successful lookup, with no cause/context/source failure, is ignored by default. HTTP404, surfaced native body/decode/transport failures, ambiguity and nested404 causes remain errors. Native LIST body ownership and continuation semantics are retained.
  Tests: `image/delete_contracts_test.go:TestDeleteImageExactNameResolutionAndFailures`, `image/delete_core_test.go:TestDeleteCoreNameAbsenceAndSourceFailureBoundaries`.
- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: Privately owned DELETE404 is read and closed once before ignore decisions. Clean default404 returns nil,nil; clean strict404 returns nil plus typed logical NotFound preserving a value native ErrUnexpectedResponseCode with DELETE/fixedURL/expected204/actual404/rawBody/Header. Body read/Close/context failure preserves native404 and every cause and is never suppressed or converted into NotFound-only success.
  Tests: `image/delete_contracts_test.go:TestDeleteImageNotFoundPolicyAndClassification`, `image/delete_core_test.go:TestDeleteCoreOwnedMissingPreservesBodyAndContextFailures`.
- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: Accepted DELETE204 result survives partial read, Close and context failures together with accepted ResponseError. Result and error independently own bytes and headers. Original read/Close/native status and ctx.Err/context.Cause survive request operation wrapping. Nested404 during accepted204 does not erase acknowledgement or classify the operation as missing. Owned204/404 bodies close once without replay after body failure.
  Tests: `image/delete_contracts_test.go:TestDeleteImageAcceptedProofAndCancellation`, `image/delete_core_test.go:TestDeleteCoreAcknowledgementAndErrorEvidenceAreIndependent`, `image/delete_core_test.go:TestDeleteCoreOwnedMissingPreservesBodyAndContextFailures`.
- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: Captured prefix/headers and policy use the original provider live token; original source/provider and target guards run after callbacks and Name lookup. Legitimate captured source/header selection remains fixed while malformed or replaced sources fail before DELETE. Cancellation, parent deadline, HTTPClient timeout and original transport causes remain errors; all HTTP/test handles are closed.
  Tests: `image/delete_contracts_test.go:TestDeleteImagePreparedSnapshotsAndSource`, `image/delete_contracts_test.go:TestDeleteImageAcceptedProofAndCancellation`, `image/delete_core_test.go:TestDeleteCoreNameAbsenceAndSourceFailureBoundaries`.
- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: Configured pre-body reauthentication/retry/backoff remains available for native rejected/transport failures and may resend DELETE before acceptance. DELETE redirect following is disabled. Privately handled404 intentionally bypasses native RetryFunc; neither handled404 nor accepted204 body read/Close failures are retried. Native ErrUnableToReauthenticate retains independent ErrOriginal/ErrReauth fields; its causes are inspected through these fields after errors.As because that native wrapper does not Unwrap. Configured RetryFunc changes to KeepResponseBody/JSONResponse/JSONBody/RawBody are rejected before another DELETE with ErrInvalidOption, original pre-body failure and any callback cause retained, preserving the empty request and owned-response contract. The contract does not promise a single wire packet under configured retry policy.
  Tests: `image/delete_contracts_test.go:TestDeleteImageProviderHooksAndRedirects`, `image/delete_core_test.go:TestDeleteCoreActualProtocolAndNativeRetryBoundaries`.
- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: The upper workflow accepts actual204 only;200/202/206/redirect/403/409 or other unexpected status normally uses native errors and native rejected-body ownership without an acknowledgement result. If configured RetryFunc changes OkCodes so native Request accepts another status, the workflow owns its read/Close then rejects the actual status with raw body/header and causes rather than creating acknowledgement evidence. Store deletion never falls back to whole deletion. Server-owned last-location409/protected/status/policy decisions do not trigger metadata/store discovery, wait or cache/Swift/task cleanup.
  Tests: `image/delete_contracts_test.go:TestDeleteImageWholeAndStoreRoutesAndEvidence`, `image/delete_contracts_test.go:TestDeleteImageNativeDeleteCompatibility`, `image/delete_contracts_test.go:TestDeleteImageProviderHooksAndRedirects`, `image/delete_core_test.go:TestDeleteCoreActualProtocolAndNativeRetryBoundaries`.
- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: Existing native Images.Delete keeps its error-only generated callable and native202/204 semantics, while Collection.Delete/Remove and other native bindings remain unchanged. Generator checks native Delete context/client/id/result signature, ErrResult and Image canonical ID and rejects signature/result/identity drift. Upper whole/store guide adds no native operation, model, inventory row or store collection.
  Tests: `image/delete_contracts_test.go:TestDeleteImageNativeDeleteCompatibility`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_delete_test.go:TestGlanceDeletePreservesNativeAPIAndDocumentsStoreWorkflow`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_delete_test.go:TestGlanceDeleteRejectsNativeSignatureResultAndIdentityDrift`.
- Owned DeleteImageRecord selects one literal image ID or an SDK-produced ImageRecord's private raw current canonical id. Public projected id, Wire, Envelope, Header, passive URLs and property id do not retarget deletion. Explicit StoreID or StoreRecord.Resource.Body canonical id selects only the store route, without name fallback. Both image/store forms, missing/null/non-string identities, handcrafted ImageRecord without private state, nonblank/UTF8/control/dot validation failures are rejected before HTTP. Raw JSON identity decoding rejects unpaired escaped surrogates without treating literal U+FFFD, valid pairs or a literal backslash-u sequence as malformed. Valid identities are escaped once as a single path segment; identity and all caller channels are copied before callbacks.
  Tests: `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordLiteralWholeAndStoreRoutesOwnOpaqueAcknowledgements`, `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordCompletePreflightRejectsAmbiguousOrUnsafeSelectors`, `image/record_identity_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordIdentityRejectsUnpairedPrivateCurrentID`, `image/record_identity_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordIdentityRejectsUnpairedStoreRecordID`, `image/record_identity_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordIdentityPreservesValidUnicodeTargets`.
- Owned whole deletion prepares the private current raw image body into the full65-field descriptor view with this call's current location before HTTP. It resets the plain ImportMethods attribute during preparation and consumes only the accepted DELETE response ImportMethods header afterward. A successful result returns an independent Record preserving current/original/dirty Body state and its prior fetched Wire/Envelope/Header/StatusCode plus passive metadata. Opaque DELETE bytes do not overlay attributes or clean pending Body. Literal-ID construction has no fabricated earlier fetch receipt; the actual DELETE receipt belongs only to Acknowledgement.
  Tests: `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordProjectsPrivateBodyAndPreservesFetchedReceipt`, `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordKeepsPendingChangesForExplicitSubsequentCommit`, `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordLiteralWholeAndStoreRoutesOwnOpaqueAcknowledgements`.
- Owned store deletion extracts image/store canonical identities only and sends a fixed /stores/{storeID}/{imageID} DELETE. It performs no whole Image descriptor projection, response Image translation or ImportMethods update, and returns Record=nil even on success. Unrelated invalid raw Image descriptors or Store properties/description do not block that route; the same invalid Image projection still blocks whole deletion before HTTP. No discovery, metadata GET, alternate name lookup or whole-delete fallback is added.
  Tests: `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordStoreOnlyExtractsIdentitiesBeforeProjection`.
- Owned concrete deletion options snapshot maps/pointers/StoreRecords and the option slice. FullOpts replaces all configuration; StoreID and StoreRecord helpers clear the other selector, and nil StoreRecord or empty full configuration explicitly restores whole mode. Explicit empty StoreID remains invalid. Ordinary headers merge, IgnoreMissing changes only its policy and callbacks apply once to independently copied configurations. Captured source/location/options and child identity stay fixed with the original provider's live token. Context/source/binding/outer guard failure stops later restoring callbacks and retains the original cause.
  Tests: `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordStoreSelectorsAndFactorySnapshots`, `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordSourceOptionsLocationAndChildSnapshots`, `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordStopsAfterGuardFailureInOptions`, `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordCompletePreflightRejectsAmbiguousOrUnsafeSelectors`.
- Owned whole/store DELETE accepts actual200..399 and preserves an opaque actual acknowledgement with fixed chosen ImageID/StoreID and independent Body/Header/StatusCode. JSON null, arrays, malformed JSON and invalidUTF8 bytes are never decoded into Image data. Accepted Read/Close/context/source/outer guard failures return only a partial acknowledgement and error, with Record=nil, physical Close once, original causes and actual ResponseError evidence. Accepted handling failures are not automatically replayed or reclassified as missing because a nested cause mentions404.
  Tests: `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordAcceptedStatusesKeepOpaqueBodyWithoutTranslation`, `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordAcceptedPhysicalFailuresReturnOnlyAcknowledgement`, `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordLiteralWholeAndStoreRoutesOwnOpaqueAcknowledgements`.
- Owned nil/defaulttrue IgnoreMissing suppresses only a clean final direct native DELETE404 after owned read/Close/context/source handling succeeds. False preserves native404 proof. Configured native retry hooks remain active, including a clean final404 after prebody retry; read/Close/cancellation/source/hook failures and nested transport404 are never hidden. Ignored missing returns nil result,nil error and creates no accepted acknowledgement or proof of backend absence.
  Tests: `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordMissingPolicySuppressesOnlyCleanFinalNative404`.
- Owned rejected statuses and transport failures without an accepted response keep nil result and native status/body/header or original cause. Native prebody retry may resend the fixed empty-body DELETE and preserves live authentication/ordinary retry headers, while changed body ownership is rejected before another request and hook-expanded OkCodes cannot turn an actual out-of-policy status into a successful acknowledgement. Native hooks and the lower generated Delete binding are not replaced or mutated by this fixed owned policy.
  Tests: `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordNativeRejectionsNeverCreateAcknowledgement`, `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordNativeRetryKeepsFixedScopeAndActualStatusPolicy`.
- Connection and Service owned deletion share the existing Image/ImageV2 source binding, current call location, source headers and live authentication. Whole deletion separates the independent local descriptor Record and earlier fetch receipt from the actual deletion acknowledgement; store deletion contributes acknowledgement only. Caller mutations to a result, source receipt or later result do not alias the earlier input or another acknowledgement, and literal construction does not invent a fetched model.
  Tests: `image_record_delete_integration_test.go:TestConnectionOwnedImageDeletionSeparatesWholeRecordFromStoreAcknowledgement`, `image/image_record_delete_test.go:TestDeleteImageRecordProjectsPrivateBodyAndPreservesFetchedReceipt`.

Python/Go differences:

- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: Go uses explicit ID or exact Name Ref and concrete owned options. Pinned Python accepts ID or mutable Image/Store Resource objects and chooses store only when truthy; its empty/falsy store broadens to whole image, whereas explicit empty Go StoreID is invalid before HTTP.
- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: Pinned Python Proxy defaults ignore_missing=True, forwards it to Store leaf whose direct default isFalse, and discards both leaf return values. Go exposes actual204 raw Body/Header/StatusCode with fixed scope IDs; ignored clean404 or logical absence returns nil,nil and carries no success acknowledgement.
- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: Pinned Python exceptions.raise_from_response accepts status<400. Existing native images.Delete accepts202/204; upper DeleteImage deliberately accepts204 only. Unexpected statuses retain native error ownership rather than full Python response translation/session parity.
- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: Go privately handles404 to read/Close before suppression, retaining read/Close/context/native404 causes and preventing nested404 failure suppression. The handled404 bypasses native RetryFunc, an explicit difference from the rejected native404 hook; other configured pre-body reauth/retry/backoff remains.
- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: Go captured route/header/options and live original provider token, source guards, redirect rejection, independently owned204 result/error proof and errors.Is/As causes are concrete workflow policies. Python Resource/adapter/session and mutable request preparation are broader; Name LIST ownership and continuation retain existing native pager behavior.
- Legacy typed DeleteImage profile only: Go bounds native RetryFunc request-option mutation: changing KeepResponseBody, JSONResponse, JSONBody or RawBody fails before another DELETE while preserving the original rejected-request error and callback cause. This deliberate body-ownership guard is not complete arbitrary Python adapter/session hook parity.
- Owned DeleteImageRecord maps the finite immutable JSON-domain ImageRecord/StoreRecord convenience profile and returns independent local Record/actual acknowledgement instead of the pinned public proxy's None. Only SDK-produced private Image raw state is accepted; arbitrary mutable Resource/Munch/subclass, same-instance changes, dirty Header components and dynamic base_path/requires_id descriptor lifecycle remain SDK-R1. Adapter cache invalidation remains SDK-C1 and full dynamic session/microversion/exception/class/transport policy remains SDK-S1 in docs/implementation-plan.md; these global tasks are not closed by this mapping.
- Python branches on store truthiness: an empty string can select whole deletion while an ordinary Store Resource can stay truthy without a usable id. Its utils.urljoin performs falsey replacement, stringification and edge-slash trimming without identity escaping. Go requires nonblank literal canonical strings, rejects explicit empty/missing/null stores and ambiguous selectors, decodes raw JSON identities without silent lone-surrogate replacement, and escapes accepted UTF8 segments once. Arbitrary Python falsy/object/coercion/JSON-parser behavior is not runtime-equivalent.
- Pinned whole deletion reuses/prepares/translates the mutable Image with has_body=False and operation Resource headers/microversion kwargs; Store.delete_image consumes only IDs and invokes session.delete(url) without those per-call kwargs or Image projection. Go preserves that state distinction but applies captured source and concrete ordinary-header/microversion transport policy to its fixed routes, an explicit extension rather than full Adapter negotiation or dirty Header parity.
- Go whole success preserves old fetched Wire/Envelope/Header/StatusCode while only ImportMethods consumes the new response header; its acknowledgement separately owns the actual200..399 DELETE bytes/status/header. Accepted read/Close/context/source failures return partial acknowledgement and no Record. These cause-safe immutable receipt policies, sticky source guards, direct clean-final-native404 classification, accepted status bounding and no automatic post-accept replay are deliberate Go choices rather than Python response/exception object equivalence or rollback guarantees.
- Cloud ImageCloudMixin.delete_image delete_objects/Swift object cleanup, cache_delete_image, task cleanup, waits, generic Collection deletion and other Source declarations are separate work. This profile introduces no name lookup, extra metadata GET, store discovery, server role check, protected/status client gate or whole fallback. Default pinned whole policy is project ADMIN_OR_PROJECT_MEMBER; optional store deletion uses project ADMIN delete_image_location, but both branches form this one declared operation and do not add a second API count. Deployment overrides/backends/locations/protection and actual deletion completion remain server-owned.
- The contract audit is pinned-source static evidence plus named local Go tests and build gates. It executes no Python SDK runtime, authenticated cloud mutation or backend authorization outcome; native provider hooks may emit multiple pre-accept DELETEs and custom transport policies remain outside the owned receipt guarantee.

- ListStores/AllStores default to basic GET info/stores with actual200 only. Local Details true chooses info/stores/detail and never becomes query; server403/404 and feature-unavailable remain errors with no basic fallback. Store embeds Metadata with actual per-page Header/status and raw canonical/unknown fields.
  Tests: `image/v2/serviceinfo/contracts_test.go:TestServiceInfoDefaultRoutesAndEvidence`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/core_test.go:TestServiceInfoCoreDiscoveryRoutesAndRawModels`.
- Store stores envelope is mandatory nonnull array and rows must be objects. Canonical id/description missing/null become empty strings, nonnull require strings. Canonical default/read-only are optional flags accepting JSON bool or lexical true/false strings; is_default/case variants are raw unknown only. Properties missing/null remain nil else raw object; optional type is string and weight exactint64, without coercion. Store IDs are passive discovery data, never derived request routes.
  Tests: `image/v2/serviceinfo/contracts_test.go:TestServiceInfoCanonicalModelsAndPresence`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/core_test.go:TestServiceInfoCoreCanonicalAndNullableDecoding`.
- Concrete defaults Details=false,Limit=0,Marker empty,MaxItems=0,Paginated=nil mean basic route, omitted wire limit/marker, uncapped allpages. Negative controls, malformed limit/marker, invalidUTF8, nil/cause-returning callbacks, body/argument/microversion and reserved query controls reject before HTTP. Wire query helper is a Go extension and does not implement Python Body filters or unknown-query discard; ordinary headers use protected shared validation.
  Tests: `image/v2/serviceinfo/contracts_test.go:TestServiceInfoCompletePreflight`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/options_test.go:TestServiceInfoOptionsRejectUnsupportedInputsBeforeHTTP`.
- List creation performs no HTTP and copies callback slice. Each iteration applies callbacks once to owned independent query/header/pointers and source snapshots. Whole typed replacement snapshots Paginated at creation/application; later leaf options replace settings in order. Retained callback handles cannot change prepared request or subsequent callbacks; standard options and iterator reuse are concurrent-safe.
  Tests: `image/v2/serviceinfo/contracts_test.go:TestServiceInfoPreparedOptionsAndLazyParallelReuse`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/options_test.go:TestServiceInfoOptionsSnapshotReplacementAndRetainedHandles`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/options_test.go:TestServiceInfoOptionsConcurrentReusableSnapshots`.
- Paginated=false stops at firstpage and MaxItems is a local raw-row cap, stops before unused row decode or next-link interpretation, and never generates a wire limit hint. Only advertised Link/body-next/HTTPLink continues, bounded to fixed origin/reverse-prefix/selectedpath/initialnonpaginationquery and cycle checks; empty page stops. Missing link ends even a nonempty response. Explicit caller limit/marker remain wire preferences but never authorize automatic marker fallback, because actual Glance discovery ignores those queries and returns the same full store set.
  Tests: `image/v2/serviceinfo/contracts_test.go:TestServiceInfoPaginationCapsAndRawMarkers`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/core_test.go:TestServiceInfoCorePagingUsesAdvertisedLinksAndStopsAtControls`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/core_test.go:TestServiceInfoCoreServerIgnoresPaginationQueries`.
- ListStores may yield valid prior rows then terminal row/page error. AllStores discards prior rows and returns nil on terminalerror; successful empty collection returns nonnil empty slice. Cap and firstpage control may stop before otherwise unused malformed rows/continuations without inventing deployment pagination support.
  Tests: `image/v2/serviceinfo/contracts_test.go:TestServiceInfoPaginationCapsAndRawMarkers`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/contracts_test.go:TestServiceInfoDefaultRoutesAndEvidence`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/core_test.go:TestServiceInfoCorePagingUsesAdvertisedLinksAndStopsAtControls`.
- Context/image client/provider/base/source headers validate before options, after callbacks and before each request/page. Capture origin/reverse prefix/provider/header policy, retain the original provider live token and reject source/provider/target replacement. Fixedrequest retains configured same-scope redirect callbacks and rejects method/origin/path/query changes before transport.
  Tests: `image/v2/serviceinfo/contracts_test.go:TestServiceInfoCompletePreflight`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/contracts_test.go:TestServiceInfoProviderSourceAndRetryBoundaries`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/core_test.go:TestServiceInfoCoreCapturesSourceAndGuardsLaterPages`.
- Accepted actual200 owns whole raw body/Header/StatusCode and closes the body once. Read/Close/JSON/full-root UTF8/model/context failures preserve ResponseError evidence and independent original/custom causes; accepted body failures do not replay. Native rejected errors and native ErrUnableToReauthenticate.ErrOriginal/ErrReauth field ownership remain unchanged. Shared DoJSON rejects RetryFunc ownership-field mutation and rechecks captured200 even when native OkCodes expands.

Generator rejects native Get/canonical model drift without inventing an upstream operation.

- Context/image client/provider/base/source headers validate before options, after callbacks and before each request/page. Capture origin/reverse prefix/provider/header policy, retain the original provider live token and reject source/provider/target replacement. Fixedrequest retains configured same-scope redirect callbacks and rejects method/origin/path/query changes before transport.

- Accepted actual200 owns whole raw body/Header/StatusCode and closes the body once. Read/Close/JSON/full-root UTF8/model/context failures preserve ResponseError evidence and independent original/custom causes; accepted body failures do not replay. Native rejected errors and native ErrUnableToReauthenticate.ErrOriginal/ErrReauth field ownership remain unchanged. Shared DoJSON rejects RetryFunc ownership-field mutation and rechecks captured200 even when native OkCodes expands.

- Exact canonical keys alone populate typed fields. Full raw root is valid UTF8 flat JSON object; unknown and case-variant fields remain raw without overriding canonical fields. Raw model Body/Header/status are independently owned, preserving unknown large numbers as json.RawMessage.

- Generated ImageV2.ServiceInfo and upper Image.API share the configured authenticated client/prefix/liveauth with Connection. Store list_only and ImportInfo service_info inventory are SDK-owned records; original native ImageImport.Get route/signature/result remains unchanged. Generator rejects native Get/canonical model drift without inventing an upstream operation.

- Owned ImportRecord adds the declared four-field view id/name/import_methods/location. Inherited id/name are nullable untyped JSON with no alternate ID; the dict descriptor preserves arbitrary nested objects and raw precision, maps nonnull nonobjects to {}, and retains null defaults. Ordered canonical/remote aliases and duplicate first-position/last-value handling match the pinned collector.
  Tests: `image/v2/serviceinfo/import_record_test.go:TestImportInfoRecordDictionaryAliasesAndDefaults`.
- ID-free query-free GET /info/import accepts actual 200..399. Empty or invalid JSON syntax yields bare declared defaults and Wire nil; valid parsed nonobjects and invalid whole UTF-8 fail with actual proof. Unknown/self/response computed location remain passive Wire and do not enter Body projection.
  Tests: `image/v2/serviceinfo/import_record_test.go:TestImportInfoRecordAcceptedToleranceAndRejectedNative`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/import_record_test.go:TestImportInfoRecordProjectionAndOwnedReceipts`.
- Resource, Wire, Envelope, Header and status are independently owned. Location is captured before option callbacks, ignores response computed location and remains fixed; header helper inputs and callback configurations snapshot independently with whole-options replacement and last-wins merges.
  Tests: `image/v2/serviceinfo/import_record_test.go:TestImportInfoRecordProjectionAndOwnedReceipts`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/import_record_test.go:TestImportInfoRecordLocationAndConcreteOptionSnapshots`.
- Original API/client/provider/endpoint/raw ResourceBase/microversion/type and outer operation guards stay sticky through options and read/Close. Accepted body faults retain actual receipt and original causes without replay; rejected native errors preserve native policy and do not fabricate a successful record.
  Tests: `image/v2/serviceinfo/import_record_test.go:TestImportInfoRecordBodyFailuresAndStickySourceGuards`, `image/v2/serviceinfo/import_record_test.go:TestImportInfoRecordAcceptedToleranceAndRejectedNative`.
- Invalid context/source/header/option input fails before HTTP and Source is validated before callbacks. Legacy strict200 GetImportInfo and the native ImageImport callable/model stay unchanged beside the new owned dictionary projection.
  Tests: `image/v2/serviceinfo/import_record_test.go:TestImportInfoRecordPreflightAndLegacyABI`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_serviceinfo_test.go:TestGlanceServiceInfoRegistryAndNativeCompatibility`.
- Both Connection Image and ImageV2 supply the same native service client, live provider authentication and captured current location to ServiceInfo. Owned import and basic stores preserve separate response proof channels across calls and pages without an extra discovery gate.
  Tests: `connection_image_serviceinfo_records_test.go:TestConnectionImageServiceInfoRecordsShareLocationAndNativeClient`.

Python/Go differences:

- The existing seven strict typed GetImportInfo contracts above remain unchanged; GetImportInfoRecord provides the pinned dictionary/default/fetch translation without replacing native ImageImport.Get.
- Go returns an independent read-only ImportRecord and concrete header helpers rather than mutable Python cached Resource identity. These helpers and explicit CloudLocation injection are Go extensions; no ID/Name/Resource/query/body is required by the pinned no-argument singleton proxy.
- Actual 200..399, whole UTF-8, protected headers, exact JSON precision and fixed-route sticky guards are Go bounds; Python JSON parser/nonfinite and binary float corner behavior may differ. Physical Close faults are errors and native rejected IO policy remains unchanged.
- Full Resource/session/cache/discovery lifecycle remains a separate whole-SDK objective. Source inspection and local HTTP tests do not establish authenticated cloud availability or deployment permission.

### `python:image/v2/add_tag`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageMutationHeaders`

- AddTag defaults to bodyless/query-free PUT on the dedicated fixed-ID endpoint; only actual204 acknowledges the request. Direct ID causes no metadata GET/list/PATCH/discovery/wait. Missing image or tag is a strict error; there is no IgnoreMissing option or nested404 suppression.

Empty/dot/dotdot/slash/backslash/control/invalidUTF8/256runes reject before Name lookup/callback/HTTP. No trim/dedupe/URL decode or tags-set replacement.

- ID is validated and routed directly; explicit Name resolves exact literal matches across existing native pages once, including UUID-shaped names, before a fixed chosen-ID mutation. Absent/ambiguous/late surfaced native lookup failures or malformed resolved IDs send no mutation. Native Name LIST model/body ownership/continuation behavior is retained.
  Tests: `image/mutation_contracts_test.go:TestImageMutationsExactNameResolutionAndFailures`, `image/mutation_core_test.go:TestImageMutationCoreExactNameAndStrictLookupFailures`, `image/mutation_contracts_test.go:TestImageMutationsFixedRoutesAndAcknowledgements`, `image/mutation_core_test.go:TestImageMutationCoreFixedRoutesAndOpaqueAcknowledgements`, `connection_image_mutations_test.go:TestConnectionImageMutationsShareClientAndKeepAcknowledgements`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_mutations_test.go:TestGlanceMutationsPreserveNativeImagesAndDocumentUpperActions`.
- Context/source/provider/type/origin/UTF8 base/source headers/version/Ref and literal tag preflight runs before caller callbacks or HTTP. Nil callbacks and callback/source/context failures preserve original causes. - Captured original client/provider/prefix and ordinary headers are used for Name LIST and mutation; the original provider latest auth token stays live. Safe source base/header changes retain captured requests, while provider replacement or foreign source retarget rejects before mutation.

- Only actual original204 creates an acknowledgement with fixed request ImageID and literal Tag or compiled Action, owned opaque Body bytes, cloned Header and actual StatusCode. Empty/nonJSON/invalidUTF8 response bytes are allowed and never decode metadata, retarget from Location/body IDs, or establish current tags/status.

- Accepted204 read/Close/context failures return retained partial/full acknowledgement plus ResponseError. Result/error Body/Header copies are independent; original read/Close/ctx.Err/custom cause survives. Accepted body closes exactly once without accepted replay. Native rejected/transport/prebody failures and unexpected200/201/202/206/404 keep nil result and their native evidence.
  Tests: `image/mutation_contracts_test.go:TestImageMutationsAcceptedFailuresAndStrictStatuses`, `image/mutation_core_test.go:TestImageMutationCoreAcceptedFailuresKeepIndependentEvidence`, `image/mutation_core_test.go:TestImageMutationCoreNativeStatusesAndPrebodyOwnership`.
- Configured native prebody retry/reauth/backoff preserves fixed method/origin/path/query and live auth, and may attempt the mutation again before acceptance. Body ownership tampering, including nil-to-JSONnull, RawBody, KeepResponseBody or JSONResponse, fails before the next attempt with original/hook/encoding causes; expanded OkCodes cannot bypass original204. Native ErrUnableToReauthenticate ErrOriginal/ErrReauth remain inspectable through fields. Changed-target/method redirects are guarded; identical-target configured redirect policy remains. Prior native APIs are preserved.
  Tests: `image/mutation_contracts_test.go:TestImageMutationsProviderHooksAndNativeCompatibility`, `image/mutation_core_test.go:TestImageMutationCoreNativeStatusesAndPrebodyOwnership`, `image/mutation_contracts_test.go:TestImageMutationsAcceptedFailuresAndStrictStatuses`, `image/mutation_core_test.go:TestImageMutationCoreAcceptedFailuresKeepIndependentEvidence`.
- AddImageRecordTag takes exactly one literal ID or supplied ImageRecord and performs PUT /images/{once-escaped-id}/tags/{once-escaped-tag} without GET/List/body/query. ID constructs 65 declared fields with tags=[], properties=null and captured current location; supplied record preserves its own location and fetch receipt.
  Tests: `image/image_record_tag_test.go:TestImageRecordTagLiteralConstructorBoundsAndCurrentLocation`, `image/image_record_tag_test.go:TestImageRecordTagOwnedSeedListSemanticsAndReceipts`, `connection_image_record_tags_test.go:TestConnectionImageRecordTagsShareOwnedSourceAndMutationReceipts`.
- After accepted HTTP, Add appends the literal tag including duplicates. Missing tags uses an independent empty list; nonnull scalar/object becomes a singleton; existing untyped raw members and caller record remain unchanged.
  Tests: `image/image_record_tag_test.go:TestImageRecordTagOwnedSeedListSemanticsAndReceipts`, `image/image_record_tag_test.go:TestImageRecordTagLiteralConstructorBoundsAndCurrentLocation`.
- The named owned tag profile accepts actual200..399 and returns independent opaque mutation bytes/header/status in Acknowledgement. Response body is never decoded as Image or tags; supplied Record Wire/Envelope/Header/StatusCode remain the original fetch evidence, while a literal-ID constructor has no fetch evidence.
  Tests: `image/image_record_tag_test.go:TestImageRecordTagAcceptsOpaque200Through399`, `image/image_record_tag_test.go:TestImageRecordTagOwnedSeedListSemanticsAndReceipts`, `connection_image_record_tags_test.go:TestConnectionImageRecordTagsShareOwnedSourceAndMutationReceipts`.
- Explicit-null tags and broken selected RawResource tags fail only after accepted HTTP. Acknowledgement remains inspectable with nil Record plus error; malformed JSON/nonUTF8 raw input is a Go-only representation boundary. Unrelated raw fields are passive, not reprojected.
  Tests: `image/image_record_tag_test.go:TestImageRecordTagNullLocalListFailsAfterAcknowledgement`.
- Input records/raw bytes, option slice and each callback config are captured independently. Existing concrete ImageMutationOpts/Header/Headers helpers run once per operation, captured route/location/source and ordinary headers are guarded, auth stays live, and ID/tag/source/context/header validation precedes callbacks or HTTP.
  Tests: `image/image_record_tag_test.go:TestImageRecordTagSourceHeaderSeedAndOptionSnapshots`, `image/image_record_tag_test.go:TestImageRecordTagPreflightAndGuardedCallbacks`, `connection_image_record_tags_test.go:TestConnectionImageRecordTagsShareOwnedSourceAndMutationReceipts`.
- Accepted Read/Close/context/source/hook failures retain possible opaque Acknowledgement and nil Record plus original causes. Rejected actual400..599 keeps nil result and native HTTP identity with physical Read/Close/cancel/binding causes. Clean native retry input stays unmodified; strict204 legacy mutation and native full-set PATCH behavior remain.
  Tests: `image/image_record_tag_test.go:TestImageRecordTagAcceptedReadCloseAndStickyGuardAcknowledgements`, `image/image_record_tag_test.go:TestImageRecordTagNativeRejectionsRetryAndLegacy204`, `image/image_record_tag_test.go:TestImageRecordTagRejectedPhysicalFaults`.

Python/Go differences:

- The original nine contracts and old AddTag/RemoveTag APIs describe the retained strict204 Ref/ACK profile. New AddImageRecordTag/RemoveImageRecordTag provides the named immutable ImageRecord profile and actual200..399 local-list behavior; legacy profile differences are not limitations of the new profile.
- Pinned public Proxy returns None while TagMixin mutates and returns the same Resource. Go returns an independent Record and separate Acknowledgement; it does not return or mutate the caller object. Full same-instance/dirty/component/default alias lifecycle remains SDK-R1.
- Pinned Proxy._get_resource(existing Image) invokes Resource._update(**{}), which resets microversion, recollects components, updates connection-derived location and to_dict. Go deliberately preserves supplied location and unrelated RawResource fields; this incidental preparation lifecycle is not reproduced by the named immutable profile (SDK-R1/S1).
- Python Body tags uses a shared mutable default list and descriptor list coercion. Go defaults are private for each call and nonnull raw scalar/object wraps in one list. Explicit null errors after HTTP as Source; malformed JSON/nonUTF8 selected raw bytes are Go-only input errors. Complete Python Munch/subclass/arbitrary descriptor/runtime behavior remains SDK-R1.
- Pinned URL join strips outer slashes and leaves inner slashes; Go validates literal identity/tag and escapes each URI segment once. Empty/invalidUTF8/control/exact dot segments reject before HTTP, while whitespace-only/nonempty slash/backslash and tags longer than255 characters remain server-owned. No schema/permission/storage conclusion is inferred.
- Go owns actual response bytes/header/status and guards captured source/route/body/options with live native auth. Full configured Python Adapter/cache/conditional GET/dynamic session/base_path/microversion behavior remains SDK-C1/S1; no accepted response replay, metadata gate, automatic wait/rollback or current full-tag fetch is added.
- The new local tags list reflects seed plus this operation, not a server-confirmed complete set. Backend persistence, deployed policy overrides/permissions/length limits and actual cloud outcomes are not proved by local fixtures or example builds.

Clean native retry input stays unmodified; strict204 legacy mutation and native full-set PATCH behavior remain.

Python/Go differences:

- The original nine contracts and old AddTag/RemoveTag APIs describe the retained strict204 Ref/ACK profile. New AddImageRecordTag/RemoveImageRecordTag provides the named immutable ImageRecord profile and actual200..399 local-list behavior; legacy profile differences are not limitations of the new profile.
- Pinned public Proxy returns None while TagMixin mutates and returns the same Resource. Go returns an independent Record and separate Acknowledgement; it does not return or mutate the caller object. Full same-instance/dirty/component/default alias lifecycle remains SDK-R1.
- Pinned Proxy._get_resource(existing Image) invokes Resource._update(**{}), which resets microversion, recollects components, updates connection-derived location and to_dict. Go deliberately preserves supplied location and unrelated RawResource fields; this incidental preparation lifecycle is not reproduced by the named immutable profile (SDK-R1/S1).
- Python Body tags uses a shared mutable default list and descriptor list coercion. Go defaults are private for each call and nonnull raw scalar/object wraps in one list. Explicit null errors after HTTP as Source; malformed JSON/nonUTF8 selected raw bytes are Go-only input errors. Complete Python Munch/subclass/arbitrary descriptor/runtime behavior remains SDK-R1.
- Pinned URL join strips outer slashes and leaves inner slashes; Go validates literal identity/tag and escapes each URI segment once. Empty/invalidUTF8/control/exact dot segments reject before HTTP, while whitespace-only/nonempty slash/backslash and tags longer than255 characters remain server-owned. No schema/permission/storage conclusion is inferred.
- Go owns actual response bytes/header/status and guards captured source/route/body/options with live native auth. Full configured Python Adapter/cache/conditional GET/dynamic session/base_path/microversion behavior remains SDK-C1/S1; no accepted response replay, metadata gate, automatic wait/rollback or current full-tag fetch is added.
- The new local tags list reflects seed plus this operation, not a server-confirmed complete set. Backend persistence, deployed policy overrides/permissions/length limits and actual cloud outcomes are not proved by local fixtures or example builds.

Guard checks around callbacks stop later restoring callbacks after an observed context/source/binding/outer failure. Protected transport/auth/representation/version headers and nil callbacks fail before HTTP.

Acknowledgement/error bytes and headers are independent, physical body closes once and accepted processing failures do not automatically replay POST. Nested404 causes on accepted processing are not classified as missing and never suppress partial evidence.

- Owned Go action deliberately rejects actual400..599 with nil result and inspectable native status/body/header proof, and never ignores404. Transport failures without an accepted response retain their original causes. This is explicit Go error policy: the pinned Python Image._action calls only session.post, the public proxy discards its Response, and Proxy.request defaults raise_exc=False, so an ordinary HTTP400..599 Response returned by the default successful-transport chain can yield None rather than a Source status exception.
  Tests: `image/image_record_actions_test.go:TestImageRecordActionsNativeRejectionsIncludeMissingAndNeverAcknowledge`, `image/image_record_actions_test.go:TestImageRecordActionsNativeRetryPreservesFixedScopeLiveAuthAndOwnership`.

Changed owned body/response representation is rejected before another attempt and hook-expanded OkCodes cannot fabricate acceptance outside200..399. Connection/Service delegate through the existing shared Image/ImageV2 source, current location and operation labels, with independent Record/fetch/ack receipts and cause-safe invalid invocation. No arbitrary Python session/cache/hook runtime equivalence is inferred.
  Tests: `image/image_record_actions_test.go:TestImageRecordActionsNativeRetryPreservesFixedScopeLiveAuthAndOwnership`, `image_record_actions_integration_test.go:TestConnectionOwnedImageActionsPreserveRecordAndSeparateReceipts`, `image_record_actions_integration_test.go:TestConnectionOwnedImageActionsRejectInvalidInvocation`.

Python/Go differences:

- Principal action status difference: pinned Image._action is session.post(url) without raise_from_response or _translate_response, and both public wrappers discard the returned Response. The complete default Proxy.request chain forwards raise_exc=False; ordinary HTTP400..599 Responses can therefore produce None on successful transport, unlike Go's explicit native status errors and strict404 rejection. Owned Go accepts actual200..399 and returns an inspectable opaque acknowledgement. This is deliberately go_mapping, not supported or identical Source accepted/error-status behavior; configured session/transport exceptions remain separate.
- Legacy typed strict204 profile only: Pinned Proxy methods accept direct ID strings or mutable Image Resource objects and return None. Go uses explicit ID/Name Ref and concrete owned headers, returns actual acknowledgement evidence, and introduces exact Name lookup as a typed convenience.
- Legacy typed strict204 profile only: Go intentionally accepts actual204 only, owns accepted body bytes/headers and preserves result+ResponseError on read/Close/context failures. Complete Python response translation, adapter/session exceptions, request preparation and arbitrary hooks remain broader.
- Legacy typed strict204 profile only: Go snapshots captured route/ordinary headers/options with live original auth and enforces bounded route/source/request-body guards. Existing native Name continuation/body behavior and configured same-target redirect/prebody retry policies remain unchanged; no single wire-attempt promise under configured retries.
- Legacy typed strict204 profile only: Pinned Image._action calls session.post and neither decodes a response nor applies raise_from_response explicitly; adapter exceptions remain native to the Python session. Go deliberately requires actual204 and returns owned acknowledgement evidence without updating a local status cache.
- Owned immutable JSON-domain ImageRecord uses SDK private canonical current state or literal ID, materializes the complete65 view/current location and resets ImportMethods before the compiled action. Python may reuse and mutate the same Resource/Munch/subclass and dict backing state. Arbitrary constructor/descriptor/default/history/dirty Header/base_path/requires_id lifecycle remains SDK-R1, Adapter cache invalidation remains SDK-C1, and dynamic session/microversion/class/exception/metrics/transport policy remains SDK-S1 in docs/implementation-plan.md; these global requirements are not completed by this mapping.
- Python utils.urljoin applies falsy replacement, object stringification and edge-slash trimming; its public action accepts no per-call attrs/options and _action supplies no explicit body/headers/microversion kwargs or _prepare_request negotiation. Go bounds identities to strict validUTF8 JSON string/literal segments, rejects lone-surrogate retargeting and escapes once, while captured source and concrete ordinary-header options are explicit transport extensions. Arbitrary Python objects, mutable routing and parser/coercion behavior are not runtime-equivalent.
- Neither pinned action translates response data into the Resource. Go returns a prepared independent Record with raw pending/original state and old fetch receipts intact, ImportMethods still empty, plus a separate actual acknowledgement; response import-method/status/id/location data never refreshes that Record. Accepted Read/Close/context/source failures return partial acknowledgement with Record=nil, owned causes/bytes/headers and no automatic accepted replay. These ownership/source-guard policies differ from Python's discarded Response and are not server completion or rollback guarantees.
- Pinned deactivate/reactivate defaults are project-scoped ADMIN_OR_PROJECT_MEMBER and server-owned active/deactivated transitions, authorization, deployment policy and idempotence. Go adds no role/status preflight, IgnoreMissing, metadata GET, name lookup, wait, cache update or cleanup. No authenticated cloud, Python SDK runtime or backend mutation was executed by this audit. Public create_image, property helper, upload/stage/import/download and their separate mandatory Source graphs are not closed by these two action mappings.

Public Resource id, Wire, Envelope, Header, passive URLs and properties cannot retarget POST. Ambiguous or missing selectors, handcrafted Record without private state, null/non-string/malformed raw identity, invalidUTF8/unpaired escaped surrogates, control/dot/blank identities and invalid context/source/ordinary headers fail before HTTP. Accepted UTF8 literal identity is escaped once as a single segment; no image Name lookup or automatic metadata GET is introduced.

Plain ImportMethods resets to a nonnil empty list. The independent prepared Record preserves private original/current/dirty Body state, prior fetched Wire/Envelope/Header/StatusCode and passive timestamps/Links. Literal construction has no fabricated fetch receipt, and projection does not clean pending Body.
  Tests: `image/image_record_actions_test.go:TestImageRecordActionsProjectPrivateCurrentBodyAndPreserveFetchReceipts`, `image/image_record_actions_test.go:TestImageRecordActionsRejectUnsafeSelectorsAndDescriptorInputsBeforeHTTP`.

Actual200..399 bytes are opaque even for empty/null/array/invalid JSON/invalidUTF8. Acknowledgement records fixed chosen ImageID/Action and independent actual Body/Header/StatusCode without synthesizing a server image status.
  Tests: `image/image_record_actions_test.go:TestImageRecordActionsLiteralRoutesAndOpaqueAcceptedStatuses`.
- Owned successful action returns the prepared independent Record without translating action response Body or headers. Response id/status/location and import-method headers remain acknowledgement evidence only; local image status, prior fetch receipt, raw pending state and the preparation's empty ImportMethods are unchanged. A subsequent explicit owned Update can still commit pending Body, and caller/result channels do not alias their input or another acknowledgement.

- Owned concrete action FullOpts snapshots and replaces the full ordinary-header configuration; Header/Headers merge with their captured inputs. The option slice and each callback config/map are copied before and after one invocation, retaining custom option/context causes and preventing retained config or location callbacks from changing captured identity/options/source. Guard checks around callbacks stop later restoring callbacks after an observed context/source/binding/outer failure. Protected transport/auth/representation/version headers and nil callbacks fail before HTTP.
  Tests: `image/image_record_actions_test.go:TestImageRecordActionsSnapshotInputsOptionsHeadersAndLocation`, `image/image_record_actions_test.go:TestImageRecordActionsStopAfterGuardFailureInOptions`, `image/image_record_actions_test.go:TestImageRecordActionsRejectUnsafeSelectorsAndDescriptorInputsBeforeHTTP`.
- Owned accepted Read/Close/context/source/outer-guard failures return only a partial actual acknowledgement plus cause-safe ResponseError and Record=nil. Acknowledgement/error bytes and headers are independent, physical body closes once and accepted processing failures do not automatically replay POST. Nested404 causes on accepted processing are not classified as missing and never suppress partial evidence.
  Tests: `image/image_record_actions_test.go:TestImageRecordActionsAcceptedPhysicalFailuresReturnOnlyPartialAcknowledgement`.
- Owned Go action deliberately rejects actual400..599 with nil result and inspectable native status/body/header proof, and never ignores404. Transport failures without an accepted response retain their original causes. This is explicit Go error policy: the pinned Python Image._action calls only session.post, the public proxy discards its Response, and Proxy.request defaults raise_exc=False, so an ordinary HTTP400..599 Response returned by the default successful-transport chain can yield None rather than a Source status exception.
  Tests: `image/image_record_actions_test.go:TestImageRecordActionsNativeRejectionsIncludeMissingAndNeverAcknowledge`, `image/image_record_actions_test.go:TestImageRecordActionsNativeRetryPreservesFixedScopeLiveAuthAndOwnership`.
- Owned native prebody retry preserves the compiled method/fixed scope/empty body and original provider live auth while retaining ordinary retry headers and hook causes. Changed owned body/response representation is rejected before another attempt and hook-expanded OkCodes cannot fabricate acceptance outside200..399. Connection/Service delegate through the existing shared Image/ImageV2 source, current location and operation labels, with independent Record/fetch/ack receipts and cause-safe invalid invocation. No arbitrary Python session/cache/hook runtime equivalence is inferred.
  Tests: `image/image_record_actions_test.go:TestImageRecordActionsNativeRetryPreservesFixedScopeLiveAuthAndOwnership`, `image_record_actions_integration_test.go:TestConnectionOwnedImageActionsPreserveRecordAndSeparateReceipts`, `image_record_actions_integration_test.go:TestConnectionOwnedImageActionsRejectInvalidInvocation`.

Python/Go differences:

- Principal action status difference: pinned Image._action is session.post(url) without raise_from_response or _translate_response, and both public wrappers discard the returned Response. The complete default Proxy.request chain forwards raise_exc=False; ordinary HTTP400..599 Responses can therefore produce None on successful transport, unlike Go's explicit native status errors and strict404 rejection. Owned Go accepts actual200..399 and returns an inspectable opaque acknowledgement. This is deliberately go_mapping, not supported or identical Source accepted/error-status behavior; configured session/transport exceptions remain separate.
- Legacy typed strict204 profile only: Pinned Proxy methods accept direct ID strings or mutable Image Resource objects and return None. Go uses explicit ID/Name Ref and concrete owned headers, returns actual acknowledgement evidence, and introduces exact Name lookup as a typed convenience.
- Legacy typed strict204 profile only: Go intentionally accepts actual204 only, owns accepted body bytes/headers and preserves result+ResponseError on read/Close/context failures. Complete Python response translation, adapter/session exceptions, request preparation and arbitrary hooks remain broader.
- Legacy typed strict204 profile only: Go snapshots captured route/ordinary headers/options with live original auth and enforces bounded route/source/request-body guards. Existing native Name continuation/body behavior and configured same-target redirect/prebody retry policies remain unchanged; no single wire-attempt promise under configured retries.
- Legacy typed strict204 profile only: Pinned Image._action calls session.post and neither decodes a response nor applies raise_from_response explicitly; adapter exceptions remain native to the Python session. Go deliberately requires actual204 and returns owned acknowledgement evidence without updating a local status cache.
- Owned immutable JSON-domain ImageRecord uses SDK private canonical current state or literal ID, materializes the complete65 view/current location and resets ImportMethods before the compiled action. Python may reuse and mutate the same Resource/Munch/subclass and dict backing state. Arbitrary constructor/descriptor/default/history/dirty Header/base_path/requires_id lifecycle remains SDK-R1, Adapter cache invalidation remains SDK-C1, and dynamic session/microversion/class/exception/metrics/transport policy remains SDK-S1 in docs/implementation-plan.md; these global requirements are not completed by this mapping.
- Python utils.urljoin applies falsy replacement, object stringification and edge-slash trimming; its public action accepts no per-call attrs/options and _action supplies no explicit body/headers/microversion kwargs or _prepare_request negotiation. Go bounds identities to strict validUTF8 JSON string/literal segments, rejects lone-surrogate retargeting and escapes once, while captured source and concrete ordinary-header options are explicit transport extensions. Arbitrary Python objects, mutable routing and parser/coercion behavior are not runtime-equivalent.
- Neither pinned action translates response data into the Resource. Go returns a prepared independent Record with raw pending/original state and old fetch receipts intact, ImportMethods still empty, plus a separate actual acknowledgement; response import-method/status/id/location data never refreshes that Record. Accepted Read/Close/context/source failures return partial acknowledgement with Record=nil, owned causes/bytes/headers and no automatic accepted replay. These ownership/source-guard policies differ from Python's discarded Response and are not server completion or rollback guarantees.
- Pinned deactivate/reactivate defaults are project-scoped ADMIN_OR_PROJECT_MEMBER and server-owned active/deactivated transitions, authorization, deployment policy and idempotence. Go adds no role/status preflight, IgnoreMissing, metadata GET, name lookup, wait, cache update or cleanup. No authenticated cloud, Python SDK runtime or backend mutation was executed by this audit. Public create_image, property helper, upload/stage/import/download and their separate mandatory Source graphs are not closed by these two action mappings.

Python/Go differences:

- Pinned Python accepts Image Resource/ID and uses inherited mutable ImageLocation Resource, descriptors, dirty/cache/session/microversion behavior. Go uses explicit ID/Name Ref, concrete owned options and actual raw response evidence; explicit Name resolution is an additional convenience.
- Go retains native Name pager model/body/continuation and rejected native response policy, while owning accepted response bytes/header/status and body failures. Full Python Resource/adapter/exception/cache/transport policy remains broader.
- Python ImageLocation._max_microversion2.17 is a framework capability hint. Go preserves existing source version policy without inventing a location microversion header, capability discovery or minimum deployment support gate.
- Pinned Python image_locations returns generic Resource.list with permissive nonarray-to-one-row coercion, URI attribute overlay, descriptors/filtering/cache and generic paging policies. Go reads the finite strict barearray endpoint once and retains unknown data without inventing stable location identity or collection CRUD.
- Actual dedicated server GET returns all locations and strips id/status; it has no request paging or show_multiple_locations condition in this handler. Legacy image metadata locations exposure and dedicated GET permission policy are separate boundaries.
- Python wraps rows in generic Resource.list and coerces a non-array body into one row; Go requires the JSON array the server returns.
- Go returns typed result values with copied raw response evidence instead of a mutable Python Resource, so there is no Resource descriptor coercion, dirty-attribute tracking or seeded-object merge.
- Python raises openstack.exceptions classes; Go returns wrapped Gophercloud or SDK errors, with resource.NotFoundError or the native 404 cause where documented.

### `python:image/v2/get_images_schema`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.Service.GetImagesSchema`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithGetSchemaOpts`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithGetSchemaHeader`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithGetSchemaHeaders`

- GetImagesSchema performs bodyless/query-free GET at captured base plus fixed schemas/images, requires actual200 and no Ref/ID/Name lookup. The pinned controller uses Controller.images with raw(); reported top-level additionalProperties is absent. Plural getters return one discovery document, never resource lists or generic pagination. Returned passive Name need not equal the fixed route.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasFixedRoutesAndRawDiscovery`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreSixteenFixedBodylessRoutes`, `connection_image_schemas_test.go:TestConnectionImageSchemasShareNativeClientAndKeepDiscoveryPassive`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_schemas_test.go:TestGlanceSchemaDiscoveryPreservesNativeImageBindingsAndDocumentsManualGetters`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasCanonicalFieldsAndPassiveKeywords`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCanonicalPresenceAndIndependentRawFields`.
- Strict whole-body UTF8 and nonnull root object with explicit canonical decoder: Name missing/null nil else string; Properties/Definitions missing/null nil else object rawmaps; Required missing/null nil else array of nonnullstrings. Empty string/map/array nonnil; no case-insensitive projection/coercion/envelope unwrap. AdditionalProperties owns arbitrary valid raw JSON including false/true/object/array/string/number and explicitnull; absent alone nil. Complete Body/typed maps/AP/header independent, exact numeric lexical precision retained. Embedded Metadata.Links/CreatedAt/UpdatedAt remain nil; links/date/id/$schema/$ref/extensions/case decoys remain raw passive data with no traversal, recursive schema validation or automatic future-request policy.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasCanonicalFieldsAndPassiveKeywords`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCanonicalPresenceAndIndependentRawFields`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasFixedRoutesAndRawDiscovery`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreSixteenFixedBodylessRoutes`, `connection_image_schemas_test.go:TestConnectionImageSchemasShareNativeClientAndKeepDiscoveryPassive`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_schemas_test.go:TestGlanceSchemaDiscoveryPreservesNativeImageBindingsAndDocumentsManualGetters`.
- Source/context/service/client/provider/origin/base/ordinary headers and source version policy validated and captured before callbacks, then context and original provider identity revalidated. Concrete full replacement and header helpers snapshot input maps/options slice/percallback configs, apply once and copy again; retained handle mutations cannot change prepared policy, reusable standard helpers race clean. Nil/error callback, protected/malformed/conflicting headers and invalid source fail before HTTP with actual causes. Safe source changes retain captured target/header while original live token changes remain live; provider replacement or unsafe retarget blocked.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasPreparedOptionsAndLiveSource`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCaptureAndCompletePreflight`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsSnapshotReplacementAndLastWins`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsOwnRetainedCallbacksAndSlice`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsHeaderAuthorityAndAliases`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsParallelReusableHelpers`.
- Only original actual200 returns typed Schema with owned raw Body/Header/StatusCode. Accepted Read/Close/context/UTF8/JSON/root/canonical model failure returns nil typed result plus independent actual200 ResponseError preserving read/Close/ctx.Err/custom causes, close once and no accepted replay. Other statuses including cached304/missing404, transport or native hook failures retain native error policy; no cached result, ignoreMissing, alternate endpoint or partial model fallback.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasResponseOwnershipAndStatusPolicy`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreMalformedAcceptedResponsesKeepProof`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreAcceptedBodyFailuresAndCauses`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasCanonicalFieldsAndPassiveKeywords`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCanonicalPresenceAndIndependentRawFields`.
- Shared fixed request and DoJSON preserve configured native prebody retry/reauth/backoff and identical-target redirect policy; changed method/origin/path/query fails before transport. Original provider/bodyless response ownership remains fixed. RetryFunc nil body to JSONnull, RawBody/JSONResponse/KeepResponseBody changes reject before another GET with original/hook/encoding causes; expanded native OkCodes cannot alter captured200 acceptance. Native reauth ErrOriginal/ErrReauth remain available via original fields. Existing native image/member/task Schema URI strings remain passive data; no native schema fetch operation or generated binding fabricated.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasProviderHooksAndNativeCompatibility`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreStrictStatusAndPrebodyOwnership`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasPreparedOptionsAndLiveSource`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCaptureAndCompletePreflight`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsSnapshotReplacementAndLastWins`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsOwnRetainedCallbacksAndSlice`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsHeaderAuthorityAndAliases`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsParallelReusableHelpers`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasResponseOwnershipAndStatusPolicy`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreMalformedAcceptedResponsesKeepProof`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreAcceptedBodyFailuresAndCauses`.

Python/Go differences:

- Pinned Python openstack.image.v2.schema.Schema uses mutable inherited Resource fetch/descriptor/default/dirty/cache/adapter/session behavior with requires_id=False. Go exposes the fixed singleton method GetImagesSchema, concrete ordinary headers and a passive owned Schema response; complete Resource/cache/exception lifecycle remains broader.
- Pinned ordinary image.v2.schema.Schema declares additional_properties as a dict descriptor and properties as a dict descriptor. Definitions and required descriptors, and bool conversion for additionalProperties, belong to the different MetadefSchema class; these four named getters use ordinary Schema. Go exposes additionalProperties as independent arbitrary raw JSON and optionally projects Definitions/Required through explicit canonical shapes while retaining the actual Body, rather than reproducing Python descriptor coercion or fabricating absent values.
- Go intentionally does not alias-unmarshal embedded Metadata links/timestamps; all schema links/templates/date/identity/URL/dialect values remain raw Body data. Root schema document validation is limited to UTF8/object/canonical field shapes, not JSON Schema draft validity or recursive enforcement.
- Server raw/minimal/CollectionSchema/DictCollectionSchema represent different payloads. Custom image properties/required and optional truthy definitions/links change server documents; the client does not synthesize absent fields, flatten nested item/dictionary schemas or automatically enforce the discovered policies.
- This public schema getter has no ID, supplied Resource, query or request body argument. Proxy._get internally creates a bare Schema with requires_id=False at the declared fixed path. Go adds concrete ordinary-header replacement/merge helpers and returns a new independently owned Schema value; arbitrary supplied subclasses, mutable cached Resource identity and generic fetch/session/cache controls are separate surfaces rather than caller inputs of this named method.
- Go requires original actual HTTP200, canonical UTF-8/non-null JSON-object and declared typed-field shapes, and returns actual Body/Header/StatusCode evidence on accepted processing errors. It preserves configured native prebody retry/reauth/transport behavior and captures route/header policy instead of reproducing all Python Adapter response translation, cached304 and exception/coercion policies. Local fixture/compile checks are evidence for this client mapping, not authenticated deployment or server schema-validation policy.
- Plural schema getters still return one discovery document, with embedded URL/$ref/templates, unknown fields, identity and dates kept passive. Following those values, listing resources, CRUD, Name resolution, recursive JSONSchema validation, schema-driven permission/quota gates, wait or cleanup are outside this named getter and are not synthesized by the Go result. Completing this row does not promote inherited Resource/session or any other image/metadata getter declaration.

Python/Go differences:

- Pinned Python openstack.image.v2.schema.Schema uses mutable inherited Resource fetch/descriptor/default/dirty/cache/adapter/session behavior with requires_id=False. Go exposes the fixed singleton method GetMemberSchema, concrete ordinary headers and a passive owned Schema response; complete Resource/cache/exception lifecycle remains broader.
- Pinned ordinary image.v2.schema.Schema declares additional_properties as a dict descriptor and properties as a dict descriptor. Definitions and required descriptors, and bool conversion for additionalProperties, belong to the different MetadefSchema class; these four named getters use ordinary Schema. Go exposes additionalProperties as independent arbitrary raw JSON and optionally projects Definitions/Required through explicit canonical shapes while retaining the actual Body, rather than reproducing Python descriptor coercion or fabricating absent values.
- Go intentionally does not alias-unmarshal embedded Metadata links/timestamps; all schema links/templates/date/identity/URL/dialect values remain raw Body data. Root schema document validation is limited to UTF8/object/canonical field shapes, not JSON Schema draft validity or recursive enforcement.
- Server raw/minimal/CollectionSchema/DictCollectionSchema represent different payloads. Custom image properties/required and optional truthy definitions/links change server documents; the client does not synthesize absent fields, flatten nested item/dictionary schemas or automatically enforce the discovered policies.
- This public schema getter has no ID, supplied Resource, query or request body argument. Proxy._get internally creates a bare Schema with requires_id=False at the declared fixed path. Go adds concrete ordinary-header replacement/merge helpers and returns a new independently owned Schema value; arbitrary supplied subclasses, mutable cached Resource identity and generic fetch/session/cache controls are separate surfaces rather than caller inputs of this named method.
- Go requires original actual HTTP200, canonical UTF-8/non-null JSON-object and declared typed-field shapes, and returns actual Body/Header/StatusCode evidence on accepted processing errors. It preserves configured native prebody retry/reauth/transport behavior and captures route/header policy instead of reproducing all Python Adapter response translation, cached304 and exception/coercion policies. Local fixture/compile checks are evidence for this client mapping, not authenticated deployment or server schema-validation policy.
- Plural schema getters still return one discovery document, with embedded URL/$ref/templates, unknown fields, identity and dates kept passive. Following those values, listing resources, CRUD, Name resolution, recursive JSONSchema validation, schema-driven permission/quota gates, wait or cleanup are outside this named getter and are not synthesized by the Go result. Completing this row does not promote inherited Resource/session or any other image/metadata getter declaration.

Python/Go differences:

- The pinned public get_tasks_schema() creates a bare Schema and fetches one fixed discovery document without public inputs. GetSchemaRecord with SchemaTasks is the source-shaped owned mapping; existing GetTasksSchema remains the compatible strict typed alternative with its original actual200 policy and five preserved contracts.
- Resource exposes only the declared class fields under canonical names, including inherited untyped id/name and nullable defaults. Wire is the actual root object; Envelope/Header/StatusCode are additive independent HTTP evidence. self, location from the response, links/templates/$ref/unknown keywords remain passive Wire data; no envelope unwrapping, schema enforcement or follow-up HTTP is inferred.
- Nonnull response dict/list/bool descriptors are projected without narrowing arbitrary JSON items to native string maps/lists. Go retains exact JSON-number spelling and its common bool conversion tests exact decimal zero, so 1e-400 is true instead of false when a Python JSON backend would underflow to binary zero. requests encoding/nonfinite-number behavior is not reproduced; valid UTF8/JSON is the Go lossless domain and invalidUTF8 remains an error.
- The Record profile follows Source HTTP<400/JSON-ValueError tolerance within ordinary final200..399 responses, but parsed nonobject roots remain errors. Success returns an owned record; physical/model/source/context failures return nil record with actual receipt in the error chain. Python exception class spelling, silently ignored physical Close behavior and nonstandard JSON parser corners are deliberate Go error-policy differences.
- Connection current location is captured once for the newly created schema resource; the response has no declared project/zone fields. Python current_location uses zone=None. Go additionally preserves explicit WithCloudLocation or dependency Zone overrides; direct image.New(client) has null location and NewWithDependencies can provide known current facts without application builders.
- Concrete ordinary header options, protected representation/auth/routing policy, immutable captured targets and source checks reuse existing SDK transport. Supplied Resource/subclass, arbitrary URI/query/body, mutable Resource/session/cache and operations on resources are independent original whole-SDK surfaces, not required public inputs or blanket remaining blockers for this finite no-argument getter.
