Accepted Read/Close/context/UTF8/JSON/root/canonical model failure returns nil typed result plus independent actual200 ResponseError preserving read/Close/ctx.Err/custom causes, close once and no accepted replay. Other statuses including cached304/missing404, transport or native hook failures retain native error policy; no cached result, ignoreMissing, alternate endpoint or partial model fallback.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasResponseOwnershipAndStatusPolicy`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreMalformedAcceptedResponsesKeepProof`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreAcceptedBodyFailuresAndCauses`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasCanonicalFieldsAndPassiveKeywords`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCanonicalPresenceAndIndependentRawFields`.
- Shared fixed request and DoJSON preserve configured native prebody retry/reauth/backoff and identical-target redirect policy; changed method/origin/path/query fails before transport. Original provider/bodyless response ownership remains fixed. RetryFunc nil body to JSONnull, RawBody/JSONResponse/KeepResponseBody changes reject before another GET with original/hook/encoding causes; expanded native OkCodes cannot alter captured200 acceptance. Native reauth ErrOriginal/ErrReauth remain available via original fields. Existing native image/member/task Schema URI strings remain passive data; no native schema fetch operation or generated binding fabricated.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasProviderHooksAndNativeCompatibility`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreStrictStatusAndPrebodyOwnership`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasPreparedOptionsAndLiveSource`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCaptureAndCompletePreflight`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsSnapshotReplacementAndLastWins`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsOwnRetainedCallbacksAndSlice`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsHeaderAuthorityAndAliases`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsParallelReusableHelpers`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasResponseOwnershipAndStatusPolicy`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreMalformedAcceptedResponsesKeepProof`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreAcceptedBodyFailuresAndCauses`.
- The additive GetSchemaRecord(ctx, SchemaMetadefNamespace, ...) maps this named getter to its fixed bodyless/query-free route without identity lookup or link following. MetadefSchema seven-field view has passive id/name, nullable additional_properties/definitions/required/properties/location; nonnull additional_properties uses audited JSON truthiness, dict fields keep objects or become {}, and required preserves arbitrary-item arrays or wraps a nonarray once. Missing and explicit null remain null. Canonical/wire aliases follow dictionary insertion order; duplicate keys retain first position with last value. Actual Wire/Envelope retain original fields and numeric bytes separately from the declared Resource view and independent response headers.
  Tests: `image/schema_records_test.go:TestSchemaRecordsFixedKindsClassViewsAndOwnedWire`, `connection_image_schema_records_test.go:TestConnectionImageSchemaRecordsShareClientAndRetainBareLocation`.
- The additive Record profile accepts actual200..399 and parses an object independently of Content-Type. Empty, opaque or malformed JSON leaves bare nullable defaults with nil Wire and actual Envelope/Header/Status; parsed null/array/string/number/bool root and invalidUTF8 fail visibly. Representative accepted read/Close-with-nested404/source/cancellation failures return nil record plus actual receipt and original causes, close once and never accepted replay or alternate schema path. Rejected HTTP403 retains native method/URL/body/header evidence rather than becoming an empty resource.
  Tests: `image/schema_records_test.go:TestSchemaRecordsAcceptedToleranceAndTerminalReceipts`.
- Connection supplies one owned current-location snapshot before options/HTTP; response location/project/zone cannot replace it. Explicit Go zone overrides and standalone null remain separate from Source defaults. Configured headers and callback carriers stay owned, callback applies once with live provider token, captured image/native client bindings and selected microversion remain shared, and invalid context/service/kind/source/dependency/location or callback source/binding/cancellation/outer-guard failures stop before HTTP with their causes. Resource, Wire, Envelope and headers do not alias each other or current Connection facts.
  Tests: `image/schema_records_test.go:TestSchemaRecordsLocationOptionsAndPreflightBindings`, `connection_image_schema_records_test.go:TestConnectionImageSchemaRecordsShareClientAndRetainBareLocation`, `image/schema_records_test.go:TestSchemaRecordsFixedKindsClassViewsAndOwnedWire`.

Python/Go differences:

- The pinned public get_metadef_namespace_schema() creates a bare MetadefSchema and fetches one fixed discovery document without public inputs. GetSchemaRecord with SchemaMetadefNamespace is the source-shaped owned mapping; existing GetMetadefNamespaceSchema remains the compatible strict typed alternative with its original actual200 policy and five preserved contracts.
- Resource exposes only the declared class fields under canonical names, including inherited untyped id/name and nullable defaults. Wire is the actual root object; Envelope/Header/StatusCode are additive independent HTTP evidence. self, location from the response, links/templates/$ref/unknown keywords remain passive Wire data; no envelope unwrapping, schema enforcement or follow-up HTTP is inferred.
- Nonnull response dict/list/bool descriptors are projected without narrowing arbitrary JSON items to native string maps/lists. Go retains exact JSON-number spelling and its common bool conversion tests exact decimal zero, so 1e-400 is true instead of false when a Python JSON backend would underflow to binary zero. requests encoding/nonfinite-number behavior is not reproduced; valid UTF8/JSON is the Go lossless domain and invalidUTF8 remains an error.
- The Record profile follows Source HTTP<400/JSON-ValueError tolerance within ordinary final200..399 responses, but parsed nonobject roots remain errors. Success returns an owned record; physical/model/source/context failures return nil record with actual receipt in the error chain. Python exception class spelling, silently ignored physical Close behavior and nonstandard JSON parser corners are deliberate Go error-policy differences.
- Connection current location is captured once for the newly created schema resource; the response has no declared project/zone fields. Python current_location uses zone=None. Go additionally preserves explicit WithCloudLocation or dependency Zone overrides; direct image.New(client) has null location and NewWithDependencies can provide known current facts without application builders.
- Concrete ordinary header options, protected representation/auth/routing policy, immutable captured targets and source checks reuse existing SDK transport. Supplied Resource/subclass, arbitrary URI/query/body, mutable Resource/session/cache and operations on resources are independent original whole-SDK surfaces, not required public inputs or blanket remaining blockers for this finite no-argument getter.

Embedded Metadata.Links/CreatedAt/UpdatedAt remain nil; links/date/id/$schema/$ref/extensions/case decoys remain raw passive data with no traversal, recursive schema validation or automatic future-request policy.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasCanonicalFieldsAndPassiveKeywords`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCanonicalPresenceAndIndependentRawFields`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasFixedRoutesAndRawDiscovery`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreSixteenFixedBodylessRoutes`, `connection_image_schemas_test.go:TestConnectionImageSchemasShareNativeClientAndKeepDiscoveryPassive`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_schemas_test.go:TestGlanceSchemaDiscoveryPreservesNativeImageBindingsAndDocumentsManualGetters`.
- Source/context/service/client/provider/origin/base/ordinary headers and source version policy validated and captured before callbacks, then context and original provider identity revalidated. Concrete full replacement and header helpers snapshot input maps/options slice/percallback configs, apply once and copy again; retained handle mutations cannot change prepared policy, reusable standard helpers race clean. Nil/error callback, protected/malformed/conflicting headers and invalid source fail before HTTP with actual causes. Safe source changes retain captured target/header while original live token changes remain live; provider replacement or unsafe retarget blocked.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasPreparedOptionsAndLiveSource`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCaptureAndCompletePreflight`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsSnapshotReplacementAndLastWins`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsOwnRetainedCallbacksAndSlice`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsHeaderAuthorityAndAliases`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsParallelReusableHelpers`.
- Only original actual200 returns typed Schema with owned raw Body/Header/StatusCode. Accepted Read/Close/context/UTF8/JSON/root/canonical model failure returns nil typed result plus independent actual200 ResponseError preserving read/Close/ctx.Err/custom causes, close once and no accepted replay. Other statuses including cached304/missing404, transport or native hook failures retain native error policy; no cached result, ignoreMissing, alternate endpoint or partial model fallback.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasResponseOwnershipAndStatusPolicy`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreMalformedAcceptedResponsesKeepProof`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreAcceptedBodyFailuresAndCauses`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasCanonicalFieldsAndPassiveKeywords`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCanonicalPresenceAndIndependentRawFields`.
- Shared fixed request and DoJSON preserve configured native prebody retry/reauth/backoff and identical-target redirect policy; changed method/origin/path/query fails before transport. Original provider/bodyless response ownership remains fixed. RetryFunc nil body to JSONnull, RawBody/JSONResponse/KeepResponseBody changes reject before another GET with original/hook/encoding causes; expanded native OkCodes cannot alter captured200 acceptance. Native reauth ErrOriginal/ErrReauth remain available via original fields. Existing native image/member/task Schema URI strings remain passive data; no native schema fetch operation or generated binding fabricated.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasProviderHooksAndNativeCompatibility`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreStrictStatusAndPrebodyOwnership`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasPreparedOptionsAndLiveSource`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCaptureAndCompletePreflight`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsSnapshotReplacementAndLastWins`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsOwnRetainedCallbacksAndSlice`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsHeaderAuthorityAndAliases`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsParallelReusableHelpers`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasResponseOwnershipAndStatusPolicy`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreMalformedAcceptedResponsesKeepProof`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreAcceptedBodyFailuresAndCauses`.
- The additive GetSchemaRecord(ctx, SchemaMetadefResourceTypes, ...) maps this named getter to its fixed bodyless/query-free route without identity lookup or link following. MetadefSchema seven-field view has passive id/name, nullable additional_properties/definitions/required/properties/location; nonnull additional_properties uses audited JSON truthiness, dict fields keep objects or become {}, and required preserves arbitrary-item arrays or wraps a nonarray once. Missing and explicit null remain null. Canonical/wire aliases follow dictionary insertion order; duplicate keys retain first position with last value. Actual Wire/Envelope retain original fields and numeric bytes separately from the declared Resource view and independent response headers.
  Tests: `image/schema_records_test.go:TestSchemaRecordsFixedKindsClassViewsAndOwnedWire`, `connection_image_schema_records_test.go:TestConnectionImageSchemaRecordsShareClientAndRetainBareLocation`.
- The additive Record profile accepts actual200..399 and parses an object independently of Content-Type. Empty, opaque or malformed JSON leaves bare nullable defaults with nil Wire and actual Envelope/Header/Status; parsed null/array/string/number/bool root and invalidUTF8 fail visibly. Representative accepted read/Close-with-nested404/source/cancellation failures return nil record plus actual receipt and original causes, close once and never accepted replay or alternate schema path. Rejected HTTP403 retains native method/URL/body/header evidence rather than becoming an empty resource.
  Tests: `image/schema_records_test.go:TestSchemaRecordsAcceptedToleranceAndTerminalReceipts`.
- Connection supplies one owned current-location snapshot before options/HTTP; response location/project/zone cannot replace it. Explicit Go zone overrides and standalone null remain separate from Source defaults. Configured headers and callback carriers stay owned, callback applies once with live provider token, captured image/native client bindings and selected microversion remain shared, and invalid context/service/kind/source/dependency/location or callback source/binding/cancellation/outer-guard failures stop before HTTP with their causes. Resource, Wire, Envelope and headers do not alias each other or current Connection facts.
  Tests: `image/schema_records_test.go:TestSchemaRecordsLocationOptionsAndPreflightBindings`, `connection_image_schema_records_test.go:TestConnectionImageSchemaRecordsShareClientAndRetainBareLocation`, `image/schema_records_test.go:TestSchemaRecordsFixedKindsClassViewsAndOwnedWire`.

Python/Go differences:

- The pinned public get_metadef_resource_types_schema() creates a bare MetadefSchema and fetches one fixed discovery document without public inputs. GetSchemaRecord with SchemaMetadefResourceTypes is the source-shaped owned mapping; existing GetMetadefResourceTypesSchema remains the compatible strict typed alternative with its original actual200 policy and five preserved contracts.
- Resource exposes only the declared class fields under canonical names, including inherited untyped id/name and nullable defaults. Wire is the actual root object; Envelope/Header/StatusCode are additive independent HTTP evidence. self, location from the response, links/templates/$ref/unknown keywords remain passive Wire data; no envelope unwrapping, schema enforcement or follow-up HTTP is inferred.
- Nonnull response dict/list/bool descriptors are projected without narrowing arbitrary JSON items to native string maps/lists. Go retains exact JSON-number spelling and its common bool conversion tests exact decimal zero, so 1e-400 is true instead of false when a Python JSON backend would underflow to binary zero. requests encoding/nonfinite-number behavior is not reproduced; valid UTF8/JSON is the Go lossless domain and invalidUTF8 remains an error.
- The Record profile follows Source HTTP<400/JSON-ValueError tolerance within ordinary final200..399 responses, but parsed nonobject roots remain errors. Success returns an owned record; physical/model/source/context failures return nil record with actual receipt in the error chain. Python exception class spelling, silently ignored physical Close behavior and nonstandard JSON parser corners are deliberate Go error-policy differences.
- Connection current location is captured once for the newly created schema resource; the response has no declared project/zone fields. Python current_location uses zone=None. Go additionally preserves explicit WithCloudLocation or dependency Zone overrides; direct image.New(client) has null location and NewWithDependencies can provide known current facts without application builders.
- Concrete ordinary header options, protected representation/auth/routing policy, immutable captured targets and source checks reuse existing SDK transport. Supplied Resource/subclass, arbitrary URI/query/body, mutable Resource/session/cache and operations on resources are independent original whole-SDK surfaces, not required public inputs or blanket remaining blockers for this finite no-argument getter.

- GetMetadefPropertySchema performs bodyless/query-free GET at captured base plus fixed schemas/metadefs/property, requires actual200 and no Ref/ID/Name lookup. The pinned controller uses Controller.metadef_property with raw(); reported top-level additionalProperties is false. Plural getters return one discovery document, never resource lists or generic pagination. Returned passive Name need not equal the fixed route.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasFixedRoutesAndRawDiscovery`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreSixteenFixedBodylessRoutes`, `connection_image_schemas_test.go:TestConnectionImageSchemasShareNativeClientAndKeepDiscoveryPassive`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_schemas_test.go:TestGlanceSchemaDiscoveryPreservesNativeImageBindingsAndDocumentsManualGetters`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasCanonicalFieldsAndPassiveKeywords`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCanonicalPresenceAndIndependentRawFields`.
- Strict whole-body UTF8 and nonnull root object with explicit canonical decoder: Name missing/null nil else string; Properties/Definitions missing/null nil else object rawmaps; Required missing/null nil else array of nonnullstrings. Empty string/map/array nonnil; no case-insensitive projection/coercion/envelope unwrap. AdditionalProperties owns arbitrary valid raw JSON including false/true/object/array/string/number and explicitnull; absent alone nil. Complete Body/typed maps/AP/header independent, exact numeric lexical precision retained. Embedded Metadata.Links/CreatedAt/UpdatedAt remain nil; links/date/id/$schema/$ref/extensions/case decoys remain raw passive data with no traversal, recursive schema validation or automatic future-request policy.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasCanonicalFieldsAndPassiveKeywords`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCanonicalPresenceAndIndependentRawFields`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasFixedRoutesAndRawDiscovery`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreSixteenFixedBodylessRoutes`, `connection_image_schemas_test.go:TestConnectionImageSchemasShareNativeClientAndKeepDiscoveryPassive`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_schemas_test.go:TestGlanceSchemaDiscoveryPreservesNativeImageBindingsAndDocumentsManualGetters`.
- Source/context/service/client/provider/origin/base/ordinary headers and source version policy validated and captured before callbacks, then context and original provider identity revalidated. Concrete full replacement and header helpers snapshot input maps/options slice/percallback configs, apply once and copy again; retained handle mutations cannot change prepared policy, reusable standard helpers race clean. Nil/error callback, protected/malformed/conflicting headers and invalid source fail before HTTP with actual causes. Safe source changes retain captured target/header while original live token changes remain live; provider replacement or unsafe retarget blocked.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasPreparedOptionsAndLiveSource`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCaptureAndCompletePreflight`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsSnapshotReplacementAndLastWins`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsOwnRetainedCallbacksAndSlice`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsHeaderAuthorityAndAliases`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsParallelReusableHelpers`.
- Only original actual200 returns typed Schema with owned raw Body/Header/StatusCode. Accepted Read/Close/context/UTF8/JSON/root/canonical model failure returns nil typed result plus independent actual200 ResponseError preserving read/Close/ctx.Err/custom causes, close once and no accepted replay. Other statuses including cached304/missing404, transport or native hook failures retain native error policy; no cached result, ignoreMissing, alternate endpoint or partial model fallback.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasResponseOwnershipAndStatusPolicy`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreMalformedAcceptedResponsesKeepProof`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreAcceptedBodyFailuresAndCauses`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasCanonicalFieldsAndPassiveKeywords`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCanonicalPresenceAndIndependentRawFields`.
- Shared fixed request and DoJSON preserve configured native prebody retry/reauth/backoff and identical-target redirect policy; changed method/origin/path/query fails before transport. Original provider/bodyless response ownership remains fixed. RetryFunc nil body to JSONnull, RawBody/JSONResponse/KeepResponseBody changes reject before another GET with original/hook/encoding causes; expanded native OkCodes cannot alter captured200 acceptance. Native reauth ErrOriginal/ErrReauth remain available via original fields. Existing native image/member/task Schema URI strings remain passive data; no native schema fetch operation or generated binding fabricated.
  Tests: `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasProviderHooksAndNativeCompatibility`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreStrictStatusAndPrebodyOwnership`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasPreparedOptionsAndLiveSource`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreCaptureAndCompletePreflight`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsSnapshotReplacementAndLastWins`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsOwnRetainedCallbacksAndSlice`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsHeaderAuthorityAndAliases`, `image/schemas_options_test.go:TestImageSchemasOptionsParallelReusableHelpers`, `image/schemas_contracts_test.go:TestImageSchemasResponseOwnershipAndStatusPolicy`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreMalformedAcceptedResponsesKeepProof`, `image/schemas_core_test.go:TestImageSchemasCoreAcceptedBodyFailuresAndCauses`.
- The additive GetSchemaRecord(ctx, SchemaMetadefProperty, ...) maps this named getter to its fixed bodyless/query-free route without identity lookup or link following. MetadefSchema seven-field view has passive id/name, nullable additional_properties/definitions/required/properties/location; nonnull additional_properties uses audited JSON truthiness, dict fields keep objects or become {}, and required preserves arbitrary-item arrays or wraps a nonarray once. Missing and explicit null remain null. Canonical/wire aliases follow dictionary insertion order; duplicate keys retain first position with last value. Actual Wire/Envelope retain original fields and numeric bytes separately from the declared Resource view and independent response headers.
  Tests: `image/schema_records_test.go:TestSchemaRecordsFixedKindsClassViewsAndOwnedWire`, `connection_image_schema_records_test.go:TestConnectionImageSchemaRecordsShareClientAndRetainBareLocation`.
- The additive Record profile accepts actual200..399 and parses an object independently of Content-Type. Empty, opaque or malformed JSON leaves bare nullable defaults with nil Wire and actual Envelope/Header/Status; parsed null/array/string/number/bool root and invalidUTF8 fail visibly. Representative accepted read/Close-with-nested404/source/cancellation failures return nil record plus actual receipt and original causes, close once and never accepted replay or alternate schema path. Rejected HTTP403 retains native method/URL/body/header evidence rather than becoming an empty resource.
  Tests: `image/schema_records_test.go:TestSchemaRecordsAcceptedToleranceAndTerminalReceipts`.
- Connection supplies one owned current-location snapshot before options/HTTP; response location/project/zone cannot replace it. Explicit Go zone overrides and standalone null remain separate from Source defaults. Configured headers and callback carriers stay owned, callback applies once with live provider token, captured image/native client bindings and selected microversion remain shared, and invalid context/service/kind/source/dependency/location or callback source/binding/cancellation/outer-guard failures stop before HTTP with their causes. Resource, Wire, Envelope and headers do not alias each other or current Connection facts.
  Tests: `image/schema_records_test.go:TestSchemaRecordsLocationOptionsAndPreflightBindings`, `connection_image_schema_records_test.go:TestConnectionImageSchemaRecordsShareClientAndRetainBareLocation`, `image/schema_records_test.go:TestSchemaRecordsFixedKindsClassViewsAndOwnedWire`.

Python/Go differences:

- The pinned public get_metadef_property_schema() creates a bare MetadefSchema and fetches one fixed discovery document without public inputs. GetSchemaRecord with SchemaMetadefProperty is the source-shaped owned mapping; existing GetMetadefPropertySchema remains the compatible strict typed alternative with its original actual200 policy and five preserved contracts.
- Resource exposes only the declared class fields under canonical names, including inherited untyped id/name and nullable defaults. Wire is the actual root object; Envelope/Header/StatusCode are additive independent HTTP evidence. self, location from the response, links/templates/$ref/unknown keywords remain passive Wire data; no envelope unwrapping, schema enforcement or follow-up HTTP is inferred.
- Nonnull response dict/list/bool descriptors are projected without narrowing arbitrary JSON items to native string maps/lists. Go retains exact JSON-number spelling and its common bool conversion tests exact decimal zero, so 1e-400 is true instead of false when a Python JSON backend would underflow to binary zero. requests encoding/nonfinite-number behavior is not reproduced; valid UTF8/JSON is the Go lossless domain and invalidUTF8 remains an error.
- The Record profile follows Source HTTP<400/JSON-ValueError tolerance within ordinary final200..399 responses, but parsed nonobject roots remain errors. Success returns an owned record; physical/model/source/context failures return nil record with actual receipt in the error chain. Python exception class spelling, silently ignored physical Close behavior and nonstandard JSON parser corners are deliberate Go error-policy differences.
- Connection current location is captured once for the newly created schema resource; the response has no declared project/zone fields. Python current_location uses zone=None. Go additionally preserves explicit WithCloudLocation or dependency Zone overrides; direct image.New(client) has null location and NewWithDependencies can provide known current facts without application builders.
- Concrete ordinary header options, protected representation/auth/routing policy, immutable captured targets and source checks reuse existing SDK transport. Supplied Resource/subclass, arbitrary URI/query/body, mutable Resource/session/cache and operations on resources are independent original whole-SDK surfaces, not required public inputs or blanket remaining blockers for this finite no-argument getter.

- Go accepts only the documented success status of each cache route; Python accepts any status below 400.
- Python Cache caps the negotiated microversion at 2.15; Go sends the configured Image microversion and adds no capability discovery.
- Go returns typed result values with copied raw response evidence instead of a mutable Python Resource, so there is no Resource descriptor coercion, dirty-attribute tracking or seeded-object merge.
- Python raises openstack.exceptions classes; Go returns wrapped Gophercloud or SDK errors, with resource.NotFoundError or the native 404 cause where documented.

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.Service.ClearCache`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithClearCacheOpts`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithClearCacheHeader`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithClearCacheHeaders`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithClearCacheTarget`

- ClearCache sends bodyless/query-free DELETE at captured base plus fixed cache, requiring original actual204 only. DirectID has only requested cache endpoint; explicit Name for per-ID calls uses unchanged native all-pages exact-name resolution, with no synthetic Ref/ID for singleton routes.
  Tests: `image/cache_contracts_test.go:TestImageCacheFixedRoutesAndClearTargets`, `image/cache_core_test.go:TestImageCacheCoreFixedRoutesAndActualEvidence`, `connection_image_cache_test.go:TestConnectionImageCacheSharesClientDefaultsAndPassiveMeasurements`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_cache_test.go:TestGlanceCacheManualMethodsPreserveNativeBindingsAndDocumentDefaults`.
- Acknowledgement records empty ImageID and independently owned Target pointer including default CacheBoth, plus actual opaque Body/Header/status204. Controller internal cache_deleted/queue_deleted counts are discarded by serializer and never synthesized. CacheBoth omits owned target header; CacheOnly/QueueOnly emit exact cache/queue. Only uint8 enum0/1/2 accepted.
  Tests: `image/cache_contracts_test.go:TestImageCacheFixedRoutesAndClearTargets`, `image/cache_contracts_test.go:TestImageCacheAcceptedEvidenceAndFailureRetention`, `image/cache_core_test.go:TestImageCacheCoreAcknowledgementFailuresAndMissingPolicy`.
- Source/context/client/provider/prefix/ordinary headers/ref captured and validated before callbacks. Concrete full replacement and helper input maps/optional bool/slice/percallback configs owned before/after one application; nil/error callback/protected/conflicting header invalid before HTTP. Cache-owned target header rejected in source/options on all7. Options and captured route/header stay fixed while original provider token stays live; provider replacement/unsafe retarget and postName source changes rejected.
  Tests: `image/cache_contracts_test.go:TestImageCachePreparedOptionsAndLiveSource`, `image/cache_core_test.go:TestImageCacheCoreCompletePreflightAndOwnedTargetHeader`, `image/cache_options_test.go:TestImageCacheOptionsSnapshotReplacementAndLastWins`, `image/cache_options_test.go:TestImageCacheOptionsOwnPointersCallbacksAndSlices`, `image/cache_options_test.go:TestImageCacheOptionsProtectedHeadersAndCompleteDefaults`, `image/cache_options_test.go:TestImageCacheOptionsParallelReusableHelpers`, `image/cache_core_test.go:TestImageCacheCoreCapturedSourceAndPrebodyGuards`.
- Accepted expected-status acknowledgement Read/Close/context failure retains result plus independent ResponseError bytes/Header/status and original causes; default owned404 failure has nil acknowledgement as described. Body closes once and accepted bytes are never replayed. Other actual statuses/prebody transport/hook failures retain native error policy; expanded native OkCodes cannot broaden captured acceptance.
  Tests: `image/cache_contracts_test.go:TestImageCacheAcceptedEvidenceAndFailureRetention`, `image/cache_core_test.go:TestImageCacheCoreAcknowledgementFailuresAndMissingPolicy`.
- Shared fixed request/DoJSON retain configured native prebody retry/reauth/backoff and identical-target redirects. Changed method/origin/path/query blocked before transport. Nil body cannot become JSONnull; RawBody/KeepResponseBody/JSONResponse/serialized body ownership cannot mutate before another attempt; original/hook/marshal causes retained. Native reauth ErrOriginal/ErrReauth remain in original fields. Existing native image bindings unchanged; no native cache API/inventory row fabricated.
  Tests: `image/cache_contracts_test.go:TestImageCacheProviderHooksAndCauseSafeMissing`, `image/cache_core_test.go:TestImageCacheCoreCapturedSourceAndPrebodyGuards`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_cache_test.go:TestGlanceCacheManualMethodsPreserveNativeBindingsAndDocumentDefaults`.
- Python target both also omits header. Literal both header is rejected by pinned controller although admin prose lists it; absent/empty actual wire means both. Source/option x-image-cache-clear-target aliases are reserved across all seven methods and may not bypass typed target policy.
  Tests: `image/cache_contracts_test.go:TestImageCacheFixedRoutesAndClearTargets`, `image/cache_core_test.go:TestImageCacheCoreFixedRoutesAndActualEvidence`, `connection_image_cache_test.go:TestConnectionImageCacheSharesClientDefaultsAndPassiveMeasurements`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_cache_test.go:TestGlanceCacheManualMethodsPreserveNativeBindingsAndDocumentDefaults`.
- Each cache call with default arguments sends the same bodyless request as Python: GET cache, PUT cache/{image}, DELETE cache/{image} with 404 ignored, DELETE cache without x-image-cache-clear-target for target both, GET cache/nodes/{image}, POST cache/clean and POST cache/prune, and decodes cached/queued images, node URLs and prune counts.
  Tests: `image/python_rejudge_test.go:TestPythonRejudgeImageCacheDefaultRequests`.

Python/Go differences:

- Python queue/delete/clear/clean proxy calls return None; get returns Cache Resource and nodes/prune return typing.cast JSON without runtime schema validation. Go returns actual acknowledgements or strict canonical owned typed/raw data, preserving response causes and requiring captured exact statuses.
- Python target both also omits header. Literal both header is rejected by pinned controller although admin prose lists it; absent/empty actual wire means both. Source/option x-image-cache-clear-target aliases are reserved across all seven methods and may not bypass typed target policy.
- Go accepts only the documented success status of each cache route; Python accepts any status below 400.
- Python Cache caps the negotiated microversion at 2.15; Go sends the configured Image microversion and adds no capability discovery.
- Go returns typed result values with copied raw response evidence instead of a mutable Python Resource, so there is no Resource descriptor coercion, dirty-attribute tracking or seeded-object merge.
- Python raises openstack.exceptions classes; Go returns wrapped Gophercloud or SDK errors, with resource.NotFoundError or the native 404 cause where documented.

- Legacy typed strict201/204 upload profile only: One fixed POST201 metadata creation followed by one PUT204 binary upload uses canonical response ID, defaults qcow2/bare/private, and performs no additional detail GET, queued status gate, checksum/import/Task/Swift/wait/cleanup.
  Tests: `image/upload_image_core_test.go:TestImageUploadCoreFixedRoutesAndIndependentEvidence`, `image/upload_image_contracts_test.go:TestImageUploadContractsWireAndOwnership`, `connection_image_upload_test.go:TestConnectionImageUploadFacadeOwnsPhases`, `internal/cmd/sdkgen/glance_image_upload_test.go:TestGlanceImageUploadPreservesNativeAndRegistry`.
- Legacy typed strict201/204 upload profile only: Concrete full opts replaces whole configuration; plural Fields replaces all raw overrides; singular field/typed helpers overwrite exact keys; Headers merges ordinary headers. Maps/raw bytes/slices/pointers and initialized callbacks are independently owned across reuse and callback retention.
  Tests: `image/upload_image_options_test.go:TestImageUploadOptionsFactoriesAndSnapshots`, `image/upload_image_options_test.go:TestImageUploadOptionsReplacementAndTypedPresence`, `image/upload_image_options_test.go:TestImageUploadOptionsRetainedCallbacksAndSource`, `image/upload_image_options_test.go:TestImageUploadOptionsParallelReuseAndHeaderPolicies`, `image/upload_image_contracts_test.go:TestImageUploadContractsPreflightAndSnapshots`.
- Legacy typed strict201/204 upload profile only: Flat precise JSON owns request name while forwarding other canonical/custom keys without descriptor aliases/coercion/schema/enum/readonly/range gates. Final formats must be nonempty UTF8 JSON strings; null/false/zero/negative/exact numbers remain server data.

Value is the completed JSON array of kept declared views, [] for an empty result, and Images aligns with it.

Python/Go differences:

- Python returns connected mutable v1/v2 Image Resource objects and emits deprecation warnings; Go returns independently owned ImageRecord views with actual Wire/Envelope/header/status evidence, concrete per-helper options and no warnings. No Resource identity, adapter cache, session or arbitrary transport parity is claimed.
- Headers are a Go extension applied to every request of the logical call. Arguments that the pinned Cloud declaration does not have are rejected before HTTP instead of becoming Python TypeError, and option callbacks run once with context/source checks between them.
- The Go image Service is Glance v2 only, so supports_version(image, '2') is always the v2 branch. This owned profile requires paired Unicode JSON status strings; Python str.lower() also accepts lone surrogates.

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordQueryHeader`

- GetImageRecordByID delegates to one strict literal GetImageRecord without query, Find fallback, missing-as-nil or deleted filtering; native 404 remains an error named GetImageRecordByID without nesting the delegated operation, and the Connection location is projected.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordGetByIDIsStrictLiteralGet`, `connection_image_cloud_records_test.go:TestConnectionCloudImageRecordsShareSourceLocationAndLiveToken`.
- Only headers are accepted. Filters, show_all, blank IDs and nil options fail before HTTP, and each option runs as one checked Get option so cancellation stops later callbacks.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordArgumentsFailBeforeHTTP`, `image/image_record_cloud_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordOptionsCancelBeforeLaterCallbacks`.

Python/Go differences:

- Python returns connected mutable v1/v2 Image Resource objects and emits deprecation warnings; Go returns independently owned ImageRecord views with actual Wire/Envelope/header/status evidence, concrete per-helper options and no warnings. No Resource identity, adapter cache, session or arbitrary transport parity is claimed.
- Headers are a Go extension applied to every request of the logical call. Arguments that the pinned Cloud declaration does not have are rejected before HTTP instead of becoming Python TypeError, and option callbacks run once with context/source checks between them.
- Go accepts a literal string ID only and rejects blank, dot-segment and control identities before HTTP; it does not accept a Resource seed or Attributes like the Proxy GetImageRecord request.

### `python:cloud/_image/ImageCloudMixin/get_image_exclude`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.Service.GetImageRecordExclude`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordQueryOpts`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordQueryHeader`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordQueryHeaders`

- The helper completes the default filter-free search_images inventory first, so deleted rows are never candidates and an empty identifier selects every row. An empty exclude returns the first selected row; otherwise rows are visited in order and the first whose name does not contain exclude is selected without inspecting later rows.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordExcludeMembershipOrderAndValues`, `image/image_record_cloud_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordSearchSelectionAndExpressions`.
- Exclude membership follows Python `in` on the raw name: substring for strings, string element equality for lists and key membership for dictionaries. A reached null, number or bool name fails with the actual list-page ResponseError and retained Inventory, while unreached rows are not inspected. Absent selection returns nil Image and Value with the consumed Inventory.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordExcludeMembershipOrderAndValues`.
- Filters, show_all, filter_deleted and invalid UTF-8 exclude fail before HTTP.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordExcludeArgumentsFailBeforeHTTP`.
- Value is the selected row's declared Resource view, including Connection location.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordExcludeMembershipOrderAndValues`.

Python/Go differences:

- Python returns a connected mutable Image Resource or its raw attribute; Go returns an owned CloudImageRecordResult whose Value is the Source return value and whose Image keeps actual Wire/Envelope/header/status evidence. No Resource identity, adapter cache or session parity is claimed.
- The owned profile requires paired Unicode JSON name strings for exclude membership, while Python str also admits lone surrogates. Headers are a Go extension for every request; filters and list controls are absent from the Source signature and fail before HTTP.

### `python:cloud/_image/ImageCloudMixin/wait_for_image`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.Service.WaitForCloudImageRecord`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudWaitOpts`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudWaitHeader`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudWaitHeaders`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudWaitTimeout`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudWaitUnlimited`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudWaitPollInterval`

- WaitForCloudImageRecord reads only the supplied record's string id and ignores its cached status. Each iteration repeats the owned Proxy find_image lookup with ignore_missing true and shared headers/location: a missing image, case-different status, null or non-string status keeps polling, exact active returns that record and exact error returns FailedStateError with the last actual receipt.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_wait_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordWaitPollsFindUntilExactActive`, `image/image_record_cloud_wait_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordWaitFailuresKeepLastRecord`.
- The iterate_timeout policy defaults to 3600 seconds and a two-second interval, checks the deadline before each lookup, expires zero or negative timeouts before HTTP and supports unlimited waiting. Timeout, error state and caller cancellation during the sleep return Lookups and the last found record with its receipt; a terminal find error stops polling immediately.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_wait_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordWaitFailuresKeepLastRecord`.
- Nil, numeric or blank ids, nil options, nonpositive poll intervals and contradictory unlimited/timeout options fail before HTTP.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_wait_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordWaitInputFailsBeforeHTTP`.

Python/Go differences:

- Python accepts any mapping with an id and raises ResourceTimeout or SDKException; Go accepts an owned ImageRecord with a nonblank string id and returns ImageRecordCloudWaitResult with context.DeadlineExceeded or FailedStateError plus the last actual receipt.
- The fixed two-second Python interval is the default; a positive PollInterval and request headers are Go extensions. An in-flight lookup is not cancelled by the wait deadline, matching iterate_timeout, while caller context cancellation is observed during sleeps.

### `python:cloud/_image/ImageCloudMixin/download_image`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.Service.DownloadCloudImageRecord`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudDownloadOpts`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudDownloadHeader`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudDownloadHeaders`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudDownloadChunkSize`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudDownloadStream`

- Missing or duplicate destinations, typed nil writers, invalid filenames, blank identities, nil options and invalid headers fail before HTTP.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_download_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordDownloadStrictFindAndPreflight`.
- DownloadCloudImageRecord finds the image with the owned ignore_missing false Find graph, returning ErrNotFound without download requests when absent, then passes the found record to DownloadImageRecord: mandatory metadata GET of the found identity, binary GET of the fetched identity and default checksum verification, writing to the borrowed writer or SDK-created file. Stream is forwarded but the required destination wins; chunk size and headers reach the lower phases.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_download_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordDownloadFindsThenDownloadsToRequiredOutput`, `image/image_record_cloud_download_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordDownloadStrictFindAndPreflight`.
- A lower download failure returns Found plus the partial download result and the native cause under the DownloadCloudImageRecord operation without nesting the delegated operation.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_download_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordDownloadFindsThenDownloadsToRequiredOutput`.

Python/Go differences:

- Python returns the lower download value or raises SDKException/ResourceNotFound; Go returns ImageRecordCloudDownloadResult separating the strict Find record from the lower download phases and wraps typed errors.
- Python treats an empty output_path as present and then falls back to memory mode; Go treats an empty Filename as absent and rejects it before HTTP. Headers are a Go extension for both phases, and Proxy-only download options keep their defaults because the Cloud signature has no such arguments.

### `python:cloud/_image/ImageCloudMixin/update_image_properties`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.Service.UpdateCloudImageProperties`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordPropertiesOpts`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordProperty`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordProperties`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordPropertyMeta`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordPropertiesMetaJSON`

- UpdateCloudImageProperties selects image or name_or_id: a Record wins over both string forms, otherwise a nonempty literal ID wins over NameOrID, and NameOrID is passed on as a literal identity without any name lookup or HTTP.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_properties_test.go:TestCloudImagePropertiesSelectsImageOrNameOrIDAndDelegates`.
- The selected image is delegated unchanged to the owned UpdateImagePropertiesRecord helper with the same options, returning its Updated/Record result: cached property copy, kernel/ramdisk lookup, conversions, raw meta precedence and conditional automatic PATCH. Literal identities keep the lower helper's missing cached-properties failure before HTTP, and errors are renamed to the Cloud operation without nesting.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_properties_test.go:TestCloudImagePropertiesSelectsImageOrNameOrIDAndDelegates`, `image/image_record_properties_test.go:TestImageRecordPropertiesBooleanUsesCanonicalComparisonAndCachedSeed`, `image/image_record_properties_test.go:TestImageRecordPropertiesRawValuesPythonStringsAndMetaLast`, `image/image_record_properties_test.go:TestImageRecordPropertiesBooleanControlsPendingCommitAndRetargeting`.

Python/Go differences:

- Python's image argument may be an Image object or a string; Go separates Record and ID fields and keeps NameOrID for name_or_id. Python truthiness of an empty string is represented by empty Go strings.
- Properties and meta use the existing concrete ImageRecordPropertiesOption values instead of Python **properties and meta dictionaries; the lower helper's documented Go differences apply unchanged.

### `python:cloud/_image/ImageCloudMixin/delete_image`

Status: `go_mapping`.

Go API:

- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.Service.DeleteCloudImageRecord`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudDeleteOpts`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudDeleteHeader`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudDeleteHeaders`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudDeleteWait`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudDeleteTimeout`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudDeleteUnlimited`
- `github.com/JSYoo5B/go-openstacksdk/image.WithImageRecordCloudDeletePollInterval`

- DeleteCloudImageRecord finds the image with the ignore-missing Find graph; absence returns Deleted false without DELETE. A found record is deleted with the owned whole-image DeleteImageRecord, and a native delete failure returns Found under the Cloud operation before any Swift cleanup or wait.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_delete_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordDeleteFindsDeletesCleansAndWaits`, `image/image_record_cloud_delete_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordDeleteLateFailuresKeepPhases`.
- When the captured image_api_use_tasks value is Python-truthy, the found properties are checked for the openstack object key and then the shade key; the stored container/name is split at the first slash and deleted through objects.DeleteObject with SLO HEAD discovery and ignore-missing defaults, without image headers. Falsey or absent configuration and missing keys skip Swift.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_delete_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordDeleteFindsDeletesCleansAndWaits`.
- Listed null properties, non-string or null object locations and values without a separator fail after the image delete with the found record's receipt and no Swift request. Optional wait repeats the shared Cloud find loop until absence, and timeout keeps the last found record.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_delete_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordDeleteLateFailuresKeepPhases`, `image/image_record_cloud_delete_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordDeleteFindsDeletesCleansAndWaits`.
- Blank identities, nil options, nonpositive intervals, invalid headers and invalid captured task configuration fail before HTTP.
  Tests: `image/image_record_cloud_delete_test.go:TestCloudImageRecordDeleteInputFailsBeforeHTTP`.

Python/Go differences:

- Python returns a bool and raises SDKException, TypeError, AttributeError, ValueError or ResourceTimeout; Go returns ImageRecordCloudDeleteResult with Deleted, Found, Delete, Object and wait evidence plus typed errors.
- The pinned delete_objects argument is never read by the source body, so Go omits it. Wait polling intervals and request headers are Go extensions; headers apply to Glance requests only.

## Usage examples

Examples use the surrounding guide's service object and context unless they declare a complete program. Python examples describe the pinned comparison API.

```python
image = conn.image.find_image("ubuntu", ignore_missing=False)
for image in conn.image.images(status="active"):
    print(image.id, image.name)
```

```go
service, err := conn.Image(ctx)
if err != nil { return err }
image, err := service.Images.Find(ctx, resource.Name("ubuntu"))
if err != nil { return err }
fmt.Println(image.ID, image.SizeBytes)

for image, err := range service.Images.List(ctx, resource.WithStatus("active")) {
    if err != nil { return err }
    fmt.Println(image.ID, image.Name)
}
```

```python
with open("ubuntu.qcow2", "rb") as data:
    uploaded = conn.create_image(
        name="ubuntu",
        data=data,
        disk_format="qcow2",
        container_format="bare",
        visibility="private",
        allow_duplicates=True,
        disable_vendor_agent=False,
        use_import=False,
        wait=True,
        timeout=300,
    )
```

```go
service, err := conn.Image(ctx)
if err != nil { return err }
data, err := os.Open("ubuntu.qcow2")
if err != nil { return err }
defer data.Close()

uploaded, err := service.Upload(ctx, image.UploadImageRequest{
    Name: "ubuntu",
    Data: data,
}, image.WithWait(resource.WithTimeout(5*time.Minute)))
if err != nil {
    if uploaded != nil { fmt.Println("created image:", uploaded.ID) }
    return err
}
fmt.Println(uploaded.ID, uploaded.Status)
```

```go
uploaded, err := service.Upload(ctx, image.UploadImageRequest{
    Name: "arm-image",
    Data: data,
}, image.WithDiskFormat("raw"),
   image.WithMinDisk(0), image.WithMinRAM(512),
   image.WithVisibility(image.VisibilityShared),
   image.WithProtected(false), image.WithHidden(false),
   image.WithTags("linux", "arm64"),
   image.WithProperty("hw_architecture", "aarch64"))
if err != nil { return err }
fmt.Println(uploaded.ID)
```

```go
ready, err := service.Images.Wait(ctx, resource.ID(image.ID), "active")
if err != nil { return err }
fmt.Println(ready.Status)

if err := service.Images.Delete(ctx, resource.ID(image.ID)); err != nil {
    return err
}
```
